Motivation: 现有视频理解基准主要依赖文本规律来回答问题，模型可以正确命名一个物理事件（如"倒水""滑动""碰撞"），却无法在时间或空间上准确定位该事件。作者希望构建一个真正评估模型是否理解物理事件时空位置的接地基准，而非仅测试文本层面的语义匹配。

Method: 1) 从SSV2、YouCook2、HoloAssist和Roundabout-TAU四个视频源收集1560个基础视频片段；2) 将每个片段转换为共享的接地事件记录（含时间、空间和语义标注）；3) 从同一记录派生出三个提示家族（物理、vstar_like、neutral_rstr）；4) 在四种输入条件下测试（原始、打乱、消融、帧掩码）；5) 评估跨模型和提示家族的时空定位性能。

Result: 1) 物理提示家族在所有设置中表现最强；2) vstar_like是最清晰的非物理语义对照；3) neutral_rstr作为更难的模板化控制基线；4) 提示家族鲁棒性是选择性的而非普遍的；5) 扰动带来的性能提升集中在原始表现较弱的案例上；6) 空间定位是所有设置中最弱的环节。

Conclusion: 视频问答推理基准在报告总体准确率的同时，应额外报告物理接地、提示感知和扰动感知的诊断指标，以更全面地评估模型对物理世界的真实理解能力。

Abstract: Physical video understanding requires more than naming an event correctly. A model can answer a question about pouring, sliding, or collision from textual regularities while still failing to localize the event in time or space. We introduce a grounded benchmark for physical video understanding that extends the what--when--where evaluation structure of V-STaR to four video sources, six physics domains, three prompt families (physics, vstar_like, and neutral_rstr), and four input conditions (original, shuffled, ablated, and frame-masked). The benchmark contains 1,560 base video clips from SSV2, YouCook2, HoloAssist, and Roundabout-TAU. Each clip is first converted into a shared grounded event record, and the three query families are derived from that record. Temporal and spatial targets are shared across prompt families, while the non-physics families use deterministic family-appropriate semantic a_what targets derived from the same record. Across models and prompt families, physics remains the strongest regime overall, vstar_like is the clearest non-physics semantic comparison, and neutral_rstr behaves as a harder templated control. Prompt-family robustness is selective rather than universal, perturbation gains cluster in weak original cases, and spatial grounding is the weakest across settings. These results suggest that video Q&A reasoning benchmarks shall report physically grounded, prompt-aware, and perturbation-aware diagnostics alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [109] [Addressing Image Authenticity When Cameras Use Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21879)
*Umar Masud,Abhijith Punnappurath,Luxi Zhao,David B. Lindell,Michael S. Brown*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方法，通过训练一个图像特定的MLP解码器和模态特定的编码器，从相机直接输出的图像中恢复出未经生成式AI幻觉内容污染的原始版本，该编码器和解码器仅需180KB存储空间，可作为元数据嵌入JPEG/HEIC等标准图像格式中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模块（尤其是ISP）集成到相机硬件中，相机直接输出的图像可能包含由AI生成的非真实内容（如AI数字变焦或低光增强导致的语义改变），用户无法意识到这些内容并非真实可信。本文旨在让用户能够恢复未包含幻觉内容的原始图像版本，避免对图像内容的误读。

Method: 采用图像特定的多层感知机（MLP）解码器与模态特定的编码器协同优化。给定相机输出的图像后，通过编码器和MLP解码器恢复出在加入幻觉内容之前的图像版本。该系统是自包含的，可在拍摄后独立应用于图像，无需访问相机ISP。编码器和MLP解码器仅需180KB存储空间，可嵌入标准图像格式的元数据中。

Result: 提出的编码器和MLP解码器仅需180KB存储空间，能作为元数据嵌入JPEG和HEIC等标准图像格式中，且可在不访问相机ISP的情况下后处理恢复无幻觉的图像版本。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了相机硬件中AI模块导致的图像内容幻觉问题，提供了一种轻量级、可嵌入元数据的后处理方案，使用户能够恢复真实可信的图像内容，避免对语义被篡改的图像产生误解。

Abstract: The ability of generative AI (GenAI) methods to photorealistically alter camera images has raised awareness about the authenticity of images shared online. Interestingly, images captured directly by our cameras are considered authentic and faithful. However, with the increasing integration of deep-learning modules into cameras' capture-time hardware -- namely, the image signal processor (ISP) -- there is now a potential for hallucinated content in images directly output by our cameras. Hallucinated capture-time image content is typically benign, such as enhanced edges or texture, but in certain operations, such as AI-based digital zoom or low-light image enhancement, hallucinations can potentially alter the semantics and interpretation of the image content. As a result, users may not realize that the content in their camera images is not authentic. This paper addresses this issue by enabling users to recover the 'unhallucinated' version of the camera image to avoid misinterpretation of the image content. Our approach works by optimizing an image-specific multi-layer perceptron (MLP) decoder together with a modality-specific encoder so that, given the camera image, we can recover the image before hallucinated content was added. The encoder and MLP are self-contained and can be applied post-capture to the image without requiring access to the camera ISP. Moreover, the encoder and MLP decoder require only 180 KB of storage and can be readily saved as metadata within standard image formats such as JPEG and HEIC.

</details>


### [110] [Directional Confusions Reveal Divergent Inductive Biases Through Rate-Distortion Geometry in Human and Machine Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21909)
*Leyla Roksan Caglar,Pedro A. M. Mediano,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 人类与深度视觉模型在分类准确率相当的情况下，存在系统性方向性混淆差异。通过率失真几何框架，发现人类表现出广泛但较弱的不对称性，而深度模型则呈现稀疏、强烈的方向性崩塌。鲁棒性训练降低了全局不对称性，但无法恢复人类式的渐进相似性。不同不对称组织会以相反方向移动RD前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示传统分类准确率无法捕捉的归纳偏差差异，通过方向性混淆矩阵和率失真几何提供更精细的模型-人类行为比较框架。

Method: 在12种扰动类型下匹配人类与深度视觉模型在自然图像分类任务上的响应，量化混淆矩阵不对称性，并通过率失真（RD）框架归纳为三种几何特征（斜率β、曲率κ、效率AUC）。

Result: 人类具有广泛但弱的不对称性；深度模型呈现稀疏、强的方向性崩塌；鲁棒性训练降低全局不对称性但未能恢复人类式的强度-宽度分布特征；不同不对称组织在性能匹配时以相反方向移动RD前沿。

Conclusion: 方向性混淆与RD几何是分布偏移下归纳偏差的紧凑且可解释的标识，揭示了准确率之外的系统性行为差异。

Abstract: Humans and modern vision models can reach similar classification accuracy while making systematically different kinds of mistakes - differing not in how often they err, but in who gets mistaken for whom, and in which direction. We show that these directional confusions reveal distinct inductive biases that are invisible to accuracy alone. Using matched human and deep vision model responses on a natural-image categorization task under 12 perturbation types, we quantify asymmetry in confusion matrices and link it to generalization geometry through a Rate-Distortion (RD) framework, summarized by three geometric signatures (slope (beta), curvature (kappa)) and efficiency (AUC). We find that humans exhibit broad but weak asymmetries, whereas deep vision models show sparser, stronger directional collapses. Robustness training reduces global asymmetry but fails to recover the human-like breadth-strength profile of graded similarity. Mechanistic simulations further show that different asymmetry organizations shift the RD frontier in opposite directions, even when matched for performance. Together, these results position directional confusions and RD geometry as compact, interpretable signatures of inductive bias under distribution shift.

</details>


### [111] [Vista4D: Video Reshooting with 4D Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21915)
*Kuan Heng Lin,Zhizheng Liu,Pablo Salamanca,Yash Kant,Ryan Burgert,Yuancheng Xu,Koichi Namekata,Yiwei Zhao,Bolei Zhou,Micah Goldblum,Paul Debevec,Ning Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vista4D 提出了一种基于4D点云（3D空间+时间）的视频重摄框架，通过静态像素分割和4D重建显式保留场景内容，并利用多视图动态数据训练增强鲁棒性，实现了对真实世界动态视频在不同相机轨迹下的高质量重摄，在4D一致性、相机控制和视觉质量上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频重摄方法在处理真实世界动态视频时存在深度估计伪影、内容外观保留不足以及难以精确控制新相机轨迹的问题，亟需一种更鲁棒且灵活的解决方案。

Method: 构建4D点云表示，结合静态像素分割和4D重建来显式保留已见内容并提供丰富相机信号；同时使用重建的多视图动态数据进行训练，以增强对真实世界推理中点云伪影的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多种视频和相机路径下，相比现有基线方法，Vista4D 在4D一致性、相机控制和视觉质量上均表现更优，并能泛化到动态场景扩展和4D场景重组等实际应用中。

Conclusion: Vista4D 通过4D点云框架有效解决了动态视频重摄中的关键难题，为真实世界动态场景的视角变换提供了鲁棒且精确的解决方案，具有良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We present Vista4D, a robust and flexible video reshooting framework that grounds the input video and target cameras in a 4D point cloud. Specifically, given an input video, our method re-synthesizes the scene with the same dynamics from a different camera trajectory and viewpoint. Existing video reshooting methods often struggle with depth estimation artifacts of real-world dynamic videos, while also failing to preserve content appearance and failing to maintain precise camera control for challenging new trajectories. We build a 4D-grounded point cloud representation with static pixel segmentation and 4D reconstruction to explicitly preserve seen content and provide rich camera signals, and we train with reconstructed multiview dynamic data for robustness against point cloud artifacts during real-world inference. Our results demonstrate improved 4D consistency, camera control, and visual quality compared to state-of-the-art baselines under a variety of videos and camera paths. Moreover, our method generalizes to real-world applications such as dynamic scene expansion and 4D scene recomposition. See our project page for results, code, and models: https://eyeline-labs.github.io/Vista4D

</details>


### [112] [Context Unrolling in Omni Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21921)
*Ceyuan Yang,Zhijie Lin,Yang Zhao,Fei Xiao,Hao He,Qi Zhao,Chaorui Deng,Kunchang Li,Zihan Ding,Yuwei Guo,Fuyun Wang,Fangqi Zhu,Xiaonan Nie,Shenhan Zhu,Shanchuan Lin,Hongsheng Li,Weilin Huang,Guang Shi,Haoqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Omni是一个统一的多模态模型，原生训练于文本、图像、视频、3D几何和隐藏表示等多种模态，通过上下文展开（Context Unrolling）机制在多种模态表示间显式推理，从而聚合互补信息，在生成和理解基准上表现优异，并具备高级多模态推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型往往针对特定模态组合设计，缺乏一个真正统一且原生支持多种异构模态（包括文本、图像、视频、3D几何、隐藏表示）的模型。作者希望弥合不同模态间的鸿沟，让模型能够在一个统一的框架下同时处理和理解多种模态信息。

Method: 提出上下文展开（Context Unrolling）机制：模型在生成预测之前，显式地在多种模态表示之间进行推理，通过跨模态的迭代交互来聚合异构模态中的互补信息，从而更忠实地逼近共享的多模态知识流形。

Result: 在多模态生成和理解基准测试上取得了强劲的性能，展示了高级多模态推理能力，包括文本、图像、视频和3D几何的上下文内生成。

Conclusion: 统一的多模态原生训练和上下文展开机制能够有效聚合异构模态中的互补信息，提升下游推理的保真度，为构建真正的多模态通用智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present Omni, a unified multimodal model natively trained on diverse modalities, including text, images, videos, 3D geometry, and hidden representations. We find that such training enables Context Unrolling, where the model explicitly reasons across multiple modal representations before producing predictions. This process enables the model to aggregate complementary information across heterogeneous modalities, facilitating a more faithful approximation of the shared multimodal knowledge manifold and improving downstream reasoning fidelity. As a result, Omni achieves strong performance on both multimodal generation and understanding benchmarks, while demonstrating advanced multimodal reasoning capabilities, including in-context generation of text, image, video, and 3D geometry.

</details>


### [113] [Seeing Without Eyes: 4D Human-Scene Understanding from Wearable IMUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21926)
*Hao-Yu Hsu,Tianhang Cheng,Jing Wen,Alexander G. Schwing,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IMU-to-4D框架，仅凭日常可穿戴传感器（如耳塞、手表、智能手机）中的惯性测量单元数据，利用大语言模型实现无视觉的4D人体运动重建与粗略场景结构感知，在多个数据集上优于现有级联方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统依赖摄像头的视觉感知在隐私、安全、能效和可扩展性方面存在持续挑战，因此探索一种无需视觉、仅依靠可穿戴传感器的4D感知替代方案。

Method: 提出IMU-to-4D框架，利用少量惯性传感器数据（来自耳塞、手表或智能手机），重新利用大语言模型进行非视觉的时空理解，预测详细的4D人体运动以及粗略的场景结构。

Result: 在多种人-场景数据集上的实验表明，IMU-to-4D比最先进的级联流水线产生了更连贯且时间上更稳定的结果。

Conclusion: 仅凭可穿戴运动传感器就能支持丰富的4D场景理解，为无视觉感知提供了可行替代方案。

Abstract: Understanding human activities and their surrounding environments typically relies on visual perception, yet cameras pose persistent challenges in privacy, safety, energy efficiency, and scalability. We explore an alternative: 4D perception without vision. Its goal is to reconstruct human motion and 3D scene layouts purely from everyday wearable sensors. For this we introduce IMU-to-4D, a framework that repurposes large language models for non-visual spatiotemporal understanding of human-scene dynamics. IMU-to-4D uses data from a few inertial sensors from earbuds, watches, or smartphones and predicts detailed 4D human motion together with coarse scene structure. Experiments across diverse human-scene datasets show that IMU-to-4D yields more coherent and temporally stable results than SoTA cascaded pipelines, suggesting wearable motion sensors alone can support rich 4D understanding.

</details>


### [114] [Seeing Fast and Slow: Learning the Flow of Time in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21931)
*Yen-Siang Wu,Rundong Luo,Jingsen Zhu,Tao Tu,Ali Farhadi,Matthew Wallingford,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Steve Marschner,Wei-Chiu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督学习框架，使模型能够感知视频中的时间流速（加速/减速），并进一步利用学到的时序推理能力从嘈杂的现实数据中构建最大规模的慢动作视频数据集，进而实现速度条件化视频生成和时间超分辨率等时间控制任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视频在计算机视觉研究中占据核心地位，但时间作为一个可感知和可操控的视觉维度却鲜有被关注。现有研究缺乏对视频中时间流速的感知能力以及操控时间流逝的方法。

Method: 1) 利用视频中固有的多模态线索和时间结构，以自监督方式学习检测速度变化和估计播放速度。2) 利用学到的时序推理模型，从嘈杂的现实来源中筛选构建目前最大的慢动作视频数据集。3) 基于该数据集，开发速度条件化视频生成（按指定速度生成运动）和时间超分辨率（将低帧率模糊视频转换为高帧率精细时序细节序列）。

Result: 构建了迄今最大规模的慢动作视频数据集；实现了速度条件化视频生成和时间超分辨率，能够在指定速度下生成运动并从低帧率模糊输入恢复高帧率细节。

Conclusion: 时间是一个可学习、可操控的感知维度，这项工作为时间可控视频生成、时间法医检测以及构建理解事件随时间展开的更丰富世界模型打开了大门。

Abstract: How can we tell whether a video has been sped up or slowed down? How can we generate videos at different speeds? Although videos have been central to modern computer vision research, little attention has been paid to perceiving and controlling the passage of time. In this paper, we study time as a learnable visual concept and develop models for reasoning about and manipulating the flow of time in videos. We first exploit the multimodal cues and temporal structure naturally present in videos to learn, in a self-supervised manner, to detect speed changes and estimate playback speed. We then show that these learned temporal reasoning models enable us to curate the largest slow-motion video dataset to date from noisy in-the-wild sources. Such slow-motion footage, typically filmed by high-speed cameras, contains substantially richer temporal detail than standard videos. Using this data, we further develop models capable of temporal control, including speed-conditioned video generation, which produces motion at specified playback speed, and temporal super-resolution, which tranforms low-FPS, blurry videos into high-FPS sequences with fine-grained temporal details. Our findings highlight time as a manipulable, perceptual dimension in video learning, opening doors to temporally controllable video generation, temporal forensics detection, and potentially richer world-models that understand how events unfold over time.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Sensitivity Uncertainty Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20903)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath,Harshit R. Hiremath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出敏感性-不确定性对齐(SUA)框架，通过对齐预测不稳定性和模型不确定性来检测LLM在对抗性和模糊输入下的失败，并引入SUA-TR训练方法和弃权规则提升模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在对抗性和模糊输入下容易产生过度自信的错误预测，传统的不确定性估计方法（如熵、自一致性）无法有效区分模型是真正不确定还是因扰动导致的不稳定。作者认为对抗敏感性和模糊性反映了同一问题：预测不稳定性和模型不确定性之间的错位。

Method: 1) 定义SUA_theta(x)标量得分，捕获分布敏感性与预测熵之间的差异；2) 证明最小化其正部可约束最坏情况下的扰动风险并与校准误差相关；3) 形式化"模糊坍缩"现象；4) 提出SUA-TR训练方法（一致性正则化+熵对齐）及弃权规则。

Result: 在问答和分类等任务上，SUA比单独使用熵或自一致性更能有效识别模型失败。

Conclusion: SUA是模型无关的框架，为提升演化中大语言模型的可靠性提供了基础。

Abstract: We propose Sensitivity-Uncertainty Alignment (SUA), a framework for analyzing failures of large language models under adversarial and ambiguous inputs. We argue that adversarial sensitivity and ambiguity reflect a common issue: misalignment between prediction instability and model uncertainty. A reliable model should express higher uncertainty when its predictions are unstable; failure to do so leads to miscalibration.
  We define a scalar score, SUA_theta(x), capturing the difference between distributional sensitivity and predictive entropy. We show that minimizing its positive part bounds worst-case perturbed risk and relates to calibration error. We also formalize ambiguity collapse, where models produce overconfident outputs despite multiple valid interpretations.
  We introduce SUA-TR, a training method combining consistency regularization and entropy alignment, along with an abstention rule for safer inference. Across tasks including question answering and classification, SUA better identifies model failures than entropy or self-consistency alone.
  The framework is model-agnostic and provides a basis for improving reliability in evolving language models.

</details>


### [116] [Hidden Secrets in the arXiv: Discovering, Analyzing, and Preventing Unintentional Information Disclosure in Source Files of Scientific Preprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20927)
*Jan Pennekamp,Johannes Lohmöller,David Schütte,Joscha Loos,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了arXiv上270万份论文源代码中隐藏的敏感信息泄露问题，发现几乎所有提交都包含不必要的文件、元数据或无关内容（如注释、API密钥、Git历史），并指出现有清理工具不可靠，提出了新的清理工具ALC-NG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arXiv公开论文的LaTeX源码及其他文件，这可能导致敏感信息（如私钥、API密钥、内部协调链接等）被无意泄露。此前虽有GitHub等场景的类似问题，但缺乏对arXiv大规模、系统性的研究。

Method: 对arXiv上270万份提供源文件的提交进行系统性分析，从三个维度考察信息泄露：(1)不必要文件的包含；(2)文件中嵌入的元数据；(3)文件中的无关内容（如源代码注释）。

Result: 几乎每份arXiv提交都包含某种形式的"隐藏"信息，包括可编辑网页文档链接、API密钥、私钥以及完整的Git历史记录等。

Conclusion: 现有工具无法可靠地清理此类敏感信息。作者提出了ALC-NG工具，能够全面移除编译LaTeX论文所不需要的文件、元数据和注释，以缓解这一问题。

Abstract: Preprints are essential for the timely and open dissemination of research. arXiv, the most widely used preprint service, takes the idea of open science one step further by not only publishing the actual preprints but also LaTeX sources and other files used to create them. As known from other contexts, such as GitHub repositories, and anecdotally exemplified for arXiv, making source code publicly available risks disclosing otherwise "hidden" information. Consequently, the public availability of paper sources raises the question of how much sensitive content is (unintentionally) disclosed through them.
  In this paper, we systematically answer this question for all 2.7M arXiv submissions with available source files across three dimensions of source file-induced information disclosure: (1) inclusion of unnecessary files, (2) metadata embedded in files, and (3) irrelevant content in files such as source code comments. Our analysis reveals that nearly every arXiv submission contains some form of "hidden" information. Notable findings range from links to editable web documents for internal coordination over API and private keys to complete Git histories.
  While different tools promise to remove such information from source files, we show that they fail to reliably achieve the intended cleaning functionality. To mitigate this situation, we provide ALC-NG to comprehensively remove files, metadata, and comments that are not needed to compile a LaTeX paper.

</details>


### [117] [SafeRedirect: Defeating Internal Safety Collapse via Task-Completion Redirection in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20930)
*Chao Pan,Yu Wu,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeRedirect是一种系统级防御方法，通过重定向模型的任务完成驱动而非压制它，有效解决了前沿LLM在合法专业任务中自发产生有害内容的内部安全崩溃（ISC）问题，将平均不安全生成率从71.2%降至8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM在执行结构上需要有害内容的合法专业任务时，会以超过95%的安全失败率自发产生有害内容，即内部安全崩溃（ISC）问题，而现有输入级防御完全失效，标准系统提示防御只能部分缓解。

Method: SafeRedirect是一种系统级覆盖方法，包含三个关键组件：1）显式授予任务失败许可；2）规定确定性硬停止输出；3）指示模型保留有害占位符未解析状态。通过重定向而非压制模型的任务完成驱动力来击败ISC。

Result: 在7个前沿LLM、3种AI/ML相关ISC任务类型的单轮对话场景中，SafeRedirect将平均不安全生成率从71.2%降至8.0%，而最强的可行基线为55.0%。跨模型消融实验显示失败许可和条件特异性普遍关键。跨攻击评估证实对ISC具有最先进防御水平，对其他攻击家族的泛化性能至少与基线持平。

Conclusion: SafeRedirect通过重定向而非抑制模型的任务完成驱动，有效解决了ISC问题，在多个前沿LLM上表现优异，代码已开源。

Abstract: Internal Safety Collapse (ISC) is a failure mode in which frontier LLMs, when executing legitimate professional tasks whose correct completion structurally requires harmful content, spontaneously generate that content with safety failure rates exceeding 95%. Existing input-level defenses achieve a 100% failure rate against ISC, and standard system prompt defenses provide only partial mitigation. We propose SafeRedirect, a system-level override that defeats ISC by redirecting the model's task-completion drive rather than suppressing it. SafeRedirect grants explicit permission to fail the task, prescribes a deterministic hard-stop output, and instructs the model to preserve harmful placeholders unresolved. Evaluated on seven frontier LLMs across three AI/ML-related ISC task types in the single-turn setting, SafeRedirect reduces average unsafe generation rates from 71.2% to 8.0%, compared to 55.0% for the strongest viable baseline. Multi-model ablation reveals that failure permission and condition specificity are universally critical, while the importance of other components varies across models. Cross-attack evaluation confirms state-of-the-art defense against ISC with generalization performance at least on par with the baseline on other attack families. Code is available at https://github.com/fzjcdt/SafeRedirect.

</details>


### [118] [Adaptive Defense Orchestration for RAG: A Sentinel-Strategist Architecture against Multi-Vector Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20932)
*Pranav Pallerla,Wilson Naik Bhukya,Bharath Vemula,Charan Ramtej Kodi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Sentinel-Strategist架构，通过上下文感知的异常检测与防御策略自适应选择，在RAG系统中有效平衡安全性与检索效用，相比始终开启的防御堆栈，将上下文召回率损失从40%以上降低至接近无防御基线水平，同时消除成员推断攻击并显著降低数据投毒成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在医疗、法律等敏感领域依赖私有领域知识，面临成员推断、数据投毒和内容泄露等安全风险。简单启用所有防御手段会导致严重的效用损失（实验表明上下文召回率下降超40%），亟需一种能够在安全性与效用之间取得平衡的自适应防御方法。

Method: 提出Sentinel-Strategist架构：Sentinel组件负责检测异常检索行为，Strategist根据查询上下文选择性部署仅必要的防御策略。该方法在三个基准数据集和五个编排模型上进行评估。

Result: (1) 消除MBA风格的成员推断泄露；(2) 检索效用相比全静态防御堆栈显著恢复，接近无防御基线水平；(3) 在数据投毒攻击下，最强变体将攻击成功率降至接近零，上下文召回率恢复至无防御基线的75%以上，但鲁棒性对模型选择敏感。

Conclusion: 自适应、查询感知的防御策略能够大幅降低RAG系统的安全-效用权衡问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare and law, where they rely on private, domain-specific knowledge. This capability introduces significant security risks, including membership inference, data poisoning, and unintended content leakage. A straightforward mitigation is to enable all relevant defenses simultaneously, but doing so incurs a substantial utility cost. In our experiments, an always-on defense stack reduces contextual recall by more than 40%, indicating that retrieval degradation is the primary failure mode. To mitigate this trade-off in RAG systems, we propose the Sentinel-Strategist architecture, a context-aware framework for risk analysis and defense selection. A Sentinel detects anomalous retrieval behavior, after which a Strategist selectively deploys only the defenses warranted by the query context. Evaluated across three benchmark datasets and five orchestration models, ADO is shown to eliminate MBA-style membership inference leakage while substantially recovering retrieval utility relative to a fully static defense stack, approaching undefended baseline levels. Under data poisoning, the strongest ADO variants reduce attack success to near zero while restoring contextual recall to more than 75% of the undefended baseline, although robustness remains sensitive to model choice. Overall, these findings show that adaptive, query-aware defense can substantially reduce the security-utility trade-off in RAG systems.

</details>


### [119] [Breaking Bad: Interpretability-Based Safety Audits of State-of-the-Art LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20945)
*Krishiv Agarwal,Ramneet Kaur,Colin Samplawski,Manoj Acharya,Anirban Roy,Daniel Elenius,Brian Matejek,Adam D. Cobb,Susmit Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释性的红队测试框架，通过自适应两阶段网格搜索算法，对8个开源LLM进行激活-steering攻击，发现Llama-3系列高度脆弱（最高91%越狱率），而GPT-oss-120B表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全审计主要依赖黑盒探测，无法系统性地利用模型内部表示中的漏洞，需要一种可解释性驱动的审计方法。

Method: 采用Universal Steering (US)和Representation Engineering (RepE)两种可解释性方法，结合自适应两阶段网格搜索算法，寻找最优激活-steering系数以诱导不安全行为。

Result: Llama-3.3-70B-4bt在US下越狱率达91%、RepE下83%；GPT-oss-120B对两种方法均鲁棒；小模型（Qwen3-0.6B、Phi4-3.8B）越狱率较低，大模型更易受影响。

Conclusion: 可解释性驱动的steering方法可作为系统安全审计的有力工具，但同时也存在双重用途风险，需加强LLM内部防御机制。

Abstract: Effective safety auditing of large language models (LLMs) demands tools that go beyond black-box probing and systematically uncover vulnerabilities rooted in model internals. We present a comprehensive, interpretability-driven jailbreaking audit of eight SOTA open-source LLMs: Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, GPT-oss- 20B, GPT-oss-120B, Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-32B, Phi4-3.8B, and Phi4-14B. Leveraging interpretability-based approaches -- Universal Steering (US) and Representation Engineering (RepE) -- we introduce an adaptive two-stage grid search algorithm to identify optimal activation-steering coefficients for unsafe behavioral concepts. Our evaluation, conducted on a curated set of harmful queries and a standardized LLM-based judging protocol, reveals stark contrasts in model robustness. The Llama-3 models are highly vulnerable, with up to 91\% (US) and 83\% (RepE) jailbroken responses on Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, while GPT-oss-120B remains robust to attacks via both interpretability approaches. Qwen and Phi models show mixed results, with the smaller Qwen3-0.6B and Phi4-3.8B mostly exhibiting lower jailbreaking rates, while their larger counterparts are more susceptible. Our results establish interpretability-based steering as a powerful tool for systematic safety audits, but also highlight its dual-use risks and the need for better internal defenses in LLM deployment.

</details>


### [120] [Breaking MCP with Function Hijacking Attacks: Novel Threats for Function Calling and Agentic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20994)
*Yannis Belkhiter,Giulio Zizzo,Sergio Maffeis,Seshu Tirupathi,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对智能体AI模型的新型函数劫持攻击（FHA），通过操纵工具选择过程强制调用攻击者指定的函数。实验表明，该攻击在5种不同模型上达到70%-100%的攻击成功率，且对上下文语义和函数集合具有鲁棒性，能够产生通用的对抗性函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI的快速发展使函数调用LLM受到广泛关注，但现有研究主要集中在注入和越狱攻击上，对函数调用接口引入的新型安全漏洞探索不足。作者旨在揭示通过操纵工具选择过程来强制调用特定函数的攻击可能性，并证明此类攻击具有跨领域适用性和通用性。

Method: 提出函数劫持攻击（FHA），通过操纵智能体模型的工具选择过程，迫使模型调用攻击者指定的特定函数。该方法对上下文语义不敏感，对函数集合具有鲁棒性，并可训练生成通用对抗性函数，实现对多个查询和负载配置的跨场景攻击。

Result: 在BFCL数据集上对5种不同模型（包括指令型和推理型变体）进行实验，攻击成功率（ASR）达到70%至100%。

Conclusion: 函数劫持攻击对智能体系统构成严重安全威胁，现有的越狱和注入攻击防护措施不足以应对这种新型攻击，需要为智能体系统建立更强的防护措施和安全模块。

Abstract: The growth of agentic AI has drawn significant attention to function calling Large Language Models (LLMs), which are designed to extend the capabilities of AI-powered system by invoking external functions. Injection and jailbreaking attacks have been extensively explored to showcase the vulnerabilities of LLMs to user prompt manipulation. The expanded capabilities of agentic models introduce further vulnerabilities via their function calling interface. Recent work in LLM security showed that function calling can be abused, leading to data tampering and theft, causing disruptive behavior such as endless loops, or causing LLMs to produce harmful content in the style of jailbreaking attacks. This paper introduces a novel function hijacking attack (FHA) that manipulates the tool selection process of agentic models to force the invocation of a specific, attacker-chosen function. While existing attacks focus on semantic preference of the model for function-calling tasks, we show that FHA is largely agnostic to the context semantics and robust to the function sets, making it applicable across diverse domains. We further demonstrate that FHA can be trained to produce universal adversarial functions, enabling a single attacked function to hijack tool selection across multiple queries and payload configurations. We conducted experiments on 5 different models, including instructed and reasoning variants, reaching 70% to 100% ASR over the established BFCL dataset. Our findings further demonstrate the need for strong guardrails and security modules for agentic systems.

</details>


### [121] [VRSafe: A Secure Virtual Keyboard to Mitigate Keystroke Inference in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21001)
*Yijun Yuan,Na Du,Adam J. Lee,Balaji Palanisamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为VRSafe的新型QWERTY虚拟键盘，通过引入假阳性击键来防御VR环境中的击键推断攻击，并集成恶意登录检测器识别未经授权的登录尝试，在显著降低攻击准确率的同时保持可用的用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR应用中基于密码的认证广泛使用，但现有研究主要聚焦击键推断攻击，缺乏有效且低成本的防御策略来缓解VR中的击键推断威胁。

Method: 设计VRSafe键盘，在输入过程中向攻击者收集的信息中引入假阳性击键以混淆真实密码；同时开发恶意登录检测器，高效识别基于击键推断获取凭证的未授权登录尝试。

Result: 通过仿真实验和真实用户研究，VRSafe能显著降低击键推断攻击的准确率，同时仅带来可接受的可用性开销，检测器在时间与内存成本方面表现高效。

Conclusion: VRSafe是VR环境中抵御击键推断攻击的一种有效且经济的防御方案，在安全性与可用性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Password-based authentication is one of the most commonly used methods for verifying user identities, and its widespread usage continues in virtual reality (VR) applications. As a result, various forms of attacks on password-based authentication in traditional environments such as keystroke inference and shoulder surfing, are still effective in VR applications. While keystroke inference attacks on virtual keyboards have been studied extensively, few efforts have developed an effective and cost-efficient defense strategy to mitigate keystroke inferences in VR. To address this gap, this paper presents a novel QWERTY keyboard called \textit{VRSafe} that is resilient to keystroke inference attacks. The proposed keyboard carefully introduces false positive keystrokes into the information collected by attackers during the typing process, making the inference of the original password difficult. \textit{VRSafe} also incorporates a novel malicious login detector that can effectively identify unauthorized login attempts using credentials inferred from keystroke inference attacks with high detection rate and minimal time and memory cost. The proposed design is evaluated through both simulation experiments and a real-world user study, and the results show that \textit{VRSafe} can significantly reduce the accuracy of keystroke inference attacks while incurring a modest overhead from a usability standpoint.

</details>


### [122] [Position Paper: Denial-of-Service Against Multi-Round Transaction Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21169)
*Yuzhe Tang,Yibo Wang,Wanning Ding,Jiaqi Chen,Taesoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对以太坊交易捆绑服务（如Flashbots Bundles）的新型拒绝服务攻击。攻击者利用合约状态中的交易间依赖关系和捆绑服务的原子块包含特性，实现了隐蔽、无风险且低成本的DoS攻击。实验表明该攻击能显著降低区块构建者收入并减慢区块生产速度，论文同时提出了相应的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拒绝服务攻击设计由于无法有效应对捆绑服务独特的多轮执行模型而失效，因此需要研究针对捆绑服务的非对称拒绝服务攻击方法。

Method: 攻击者通过合约状态利用交易间依赖关系实现攻击的隐蔽性（evasiveness），并滥用捆绑服务的原子块包含（atomic block inclusion）等特性来大幅降低攻击的资本和运营成本。

Result: 实验表明该攻击具有高成功率，能够显著降低区块构建者的收入并减慢区块生产速度。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了捆绑服务面临的新型安全威胁，并提出了相应的缓解策略来防御此类攻击。

Abstract: In Ethereum, transaction-bundling services are a critical component of block builders, such as Flashbots Bundles, and are widely used by MEV searchers. Disrupting bundling services can degrade searcher experience and reduce builder revenue. Despite the extensive studies, the existing denial-of-service attack designs are ineffective against bundling services due to their unique multi-round execution model.
  This paper studies the open problem of asymmetric denial-of-service against bundling services. We develop evasive, risk-free, and low-cost DoS attacks on Flashbots' bundling service, the only open-source bundling service known to us. Our attacks exploit inter-transaction dependencies through contract state to achieve evasiveness, and abuse bundling-specific features, such as atomic block inclusion, to significantly reduce both capital and operational costs of the attack.
  Experimental results show that our attacks achieve high success rates, substantially reduce builders' revenue, and slow block production. We further propose mitigation strategies for the identified risks.

</details>


### [123] [Physically Unclonable Functions for Secure IoT Authentication and Hardware-Anchored AI Model Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21188)
*Maryam Taghi Zadeh,Mohsen Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了AI赋能IoT系统中基于硬件的信任机制，比较了TPM、PUF、混合硬件信任根及纯软件安全方案，指出硬件信任方案在抗物理篡改和设备克隆方面优于纯软件方法，而PUF和混合信任锚点为大规模AIoT系统提供了最佳的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与IoT及边缘计算的快速融合使得设备认证和AI模型完整性保障成为迫切需求，现有信任机制在资源与安全约束下面临挑战，需系统梳理和比较各类硬件根信任方案。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对TPM测量与认证、硅基/FPGA物理不可克隆函数(PUF)、混合容器感知硬件根信任以及纯软件安全方案进行系统比较分析，从安全性、可扩展性、成本和部署复杂度等维度评估权衡。

Result: 硬件根信任方案在对抗性及物理暴露环境下相比纯软件方案提供更强的防护；PUF和混合信任锚点在大规模AIoT系统中展现出最佳的安全与性能平衡；纯软件信任机制在对抗性和物理暴露场景中不足。

Conclusion: 明确了当前设计挑战，为未来可信AIoT平台开发提供指导，指出PUF和混合方案是发展方向。

Abstract: The rapid integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into Internet of Things (IoT) and edge computing systems has intensified the need for robust, hardware-rooted trust mechanisms capable of ensuring device authenticity and AI model integrity under strict resource and security constraints. This survey reviews and synthesizes existing literature on hardware-rooted trust mechanisms for AI-enabled IoT systems. It systematically examines and compares representative trust anchor mechanisms, including Trusted Platform Module (TPM)-based measurement and attestation, silicon and FPGA-based Physical Unclonable Functions (PUFs), hybrid container-aware hardware roots of trust, and software-only security approaches. The analysis highlights how hardware-rooted solutions generally provide stronger protection against physical tampering and device cloning compared to software-only approaches, particularly in adversarial and physically exposed environments, while hybrid designs extend hardware trust into runtime and containerized environments commonly used in modern edge deployments. By evaluating trade-offs among security strength, scalability, cost, and deployment complexity, the study shows that PUF-based and hybrid trust anchors offer a promising balance for large-scale, AI-enabled IoT systems, whereas software-only trust mechanisms remain insufficient in adversarial and physically exposed settings. The presented comparison aims to clarify current design challenges and guide future development of trustworthy AI-enabled IoT platforms.

</details>


### [124] [ECCFROG522PP: An Enhanced 522 bit Weierstrass Elliptic Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21261)
*Victor Duarte Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一条522位素域椭圆曲线ECCFROG522PP，所有参数通过BLAKE3确定性管道从公开种子生成，强调透明度、可审计性和可复现性，而非性能优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广泛部署的曲线如NIST P-256和secp256k1提供约128位经典安全，更高安全需求场景通常使用NIST P-521、Curve448、Brainpool P512等曲线。这些曲线的生成过程缺乏足够的透明度和可审计性，因此需要一条完全确定性生成、参数可公开验证的椭圆曲线。

Method: 采用BLAKE3哈希函数构建确定性参数生成管道，所有关键参数从固定公开种子通过公开指定的索引派生。生成的曲线具有素数阶、协因子为1、基点是确定性派生的满阶点。同时对二次扭转、嵌入度下界和CM判别式进行了标准安全检查。

Result: 成功生成了一条522位素域上的短Weierstrass形式椭圆曲线ECCFROG522PP，具备素数阶和协因子1，二次扭转具有大的已证明素因子，并提供了嵌入度下界的文档化证明以及标准安全检查结果。完整生成和验证过程可通过公开制品和参考脚本端到端复现。

Conclusion: ECCFROG522PP为与NIST P-521同等级别安全需求的场景提供了一条完全透明、可审计、可独立验证的替代曲线选择，其确定性生成过程确保了参数的可复现性和可信度。

Abstract: This paper presents ECCFROG522PP, a 522-bit prime-field elliptic curve in short Weierstrass form, designed with a focus on deterministic generation and public reproducibility. The central design principle is that all critical parameters are derived from a fixed public seed through a transparent and verifiable procedure. While many deployed systems rely on NIST P-256 and secp256k1, which target approximately 128-bit classical security, higher security applications typically consider curves such as NIST P-521, Curve448, and Brainpool P512. ECCFROG522PP is intended for the same general classical security range as P-521, with emphasis on transparency, auditability, and reproducibility rather than performance optimization.
  The curve parameters are generated through a BLAKE3-based deterministic pipeline with publicly specified indices. The resulting construction has prime order, cofactor one, and a deterministically derived base point of full order. The quadratic twist has a large proven prime factor, and the construction includes a documented lower bound on the embedding degree together with standard sanity checks against low embedding degree reductions and basic CM discriminant anomalies.
  The full generation and validation procedure can be reproduced end to end from public artifacts and reference scripts, enabling independent verification of all parameters and checks.

</details>


### [125] [Strategic Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Architecture for Cost-Effective Code Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21282)
*Zhaohui Geoffrey Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于博弈论启发的异构多智能体架构（"3+1"架构），结合三个云端LLM专家（DeepSeek-V3）和一个本地轻量级验证器（Qwen3-8B），在NIST Juliet测试集上以低成本实现高召回率（100%）和77.2%的F1分数，优于单专家基线和静态分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动代码漏洞检测方法在检测精度和计算成本之间存在根本性权衡，需要一种兼顾高精度与低成本的解决方案。

Method: 提出"3+1"异构多智能体架构：三个云端专家智能体（DeepSeek-V3）分别从代码结构、安全模式和调试逻辑三个互补视角并行分析代码，一个本地轻量级验证器（Qwen3-8B）以零边际成本进行对抗性验证。通过两层博弈框架形式化设计——专家间合作博弈（捕获超加性价值）和对抗性验证博弈（建模质量保证激励）。

Result: 在NIST Juliet测试集262个真实样本（14种CWE类型，平衡脆弱与良性类别）上：F1分数77.2%，精确率62.9%，召回率100%，每个样本成本$0.002；优于单专家LLM基线（F1 71.4%）和Cppcheck静态分析（MCC 0）；对抗验证器提升精确率+10.3个百分点（p<1e-6，McNemar检验）；并行执行实现3.0倍加速。

Conclusion: 证明了博弈论设计原则可以指导构建有效的异构多智能体架构，用于对成本敏感的软件工程任务。

Abstract: Automated code vulnerability detection is critical for software security, yet existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off between detection accuracy and computational cost. We propose a heterogeneous multi-agent architecture inspired by game-theoretic principles, combining cloud-based LLM experts with a local lightweight verifier. Our "3+1" architecture deploys three cloud-based expert agents (DeepSeek-V3) that analyze code from complementary perspectives - code structure, security patterns, and debugging logic - in parallel, while a local verifier (Qwen3-8B) performs adversarial validation at zero marginal cost.
  We formalize this design through a two-layer game framework: (1) a cooperative game among experts capturing super-additive value from diverse perspectives, and (2) an adversarial verification game modeling quality assurance incentives.
  Experiments on 262 real samples from the NIST Juliet Test Suite across 14 CWE types, with balanced vulnerable and benign classes, demonstrate that our approach achieves a 77.2% F1 score with 62.9% precision and 100% recall at $0.002 per sample - outperforming both a single-expert LLM baseline (F1 71.4%) and Cppcheck static analysis (MCC 0). The adversarial verifier significantly improves precision (+10.3 percentage points, p < 1e-6, McNemar's test) by filtering false positives, while parallel execution achieves a 3.0x speedup.
  Our work demonstrates that game-theoretic design principles can guide effective heterogeneous multi-agent architectures for cost-sensitive software engineering tasks.

</details>


### [126] [Adversarial Evasion in Non-Stationary Malware Detection: Minimizing Drift Signals through Similarity-Constrained Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21310)
*Pawan Acharya,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究能否生成同时逃避分类器检测和漂移监控的对抗性恶意软件样本，提出在标准化特征空间中生成带有相似性正则化的对抗样本，实验表明ℓ2正则化能有效降低漂移信号，但扰动预算会显著影响逃避与可检测性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在非平稳现实环境中面临持续演化的问题：恶意软件特征和检测系统都在不断变化。本文旨在探究攻击者能否生成既逃逸分类又规避漂移监控机制的对抗性恶意软件样本。

Method: 在分类器的标准化特征空间中生成定向对抗样本，引入相似性正则化器（重点考察ℓ2正则化），通过约束扰动以保持与干净恶意软件的分布相似性，构建平衡定向误分类与最小化漂移信号的优化目标。

Result: 相似性约束能有效降低输出漂移信号，其中ℓ2正则化效果最佳；扰动预算显著影响逃避与可检测性的权衡，预算增加导致攻击成功率提高但漂移指标也更为显著。

Conclusion: 对抗性恶意软件攻击可以在逃避分类的同时保持对漂移监控的隐蔽性，但存在内在权衡：攻击强度越高，被漂移检测机制发现的风险也越大。

Abstract: Deep learning has emerged as a powerful approach for malware detection, demonstrating impressive accuracy across various data representations. However, these models face critical limitations in real-world, non-stationary environments where both malware characteristics and detection systems continuously evolve. Our research investigates a fundamental security question: Can an attacker generate adversarial malware samples that simultaneously evade classification and remain inconspicuous to drift monitoring mechanisms? We propose a novel approach that generates targeted adversarial examples in the classifier's standardized feature space, augmented with sophisticated similarity regularizers. By carefully constraining perturbations to maintain distributional similarity with clean malware, we create an optimization objective that balances targeted misclassification with drift signal minimization. We quantify the effectiveness of this approach by comprehensively comparing classifier output probabilities using multiple drift metrics. Our experiments demonstrate that similarity constraints can reduce output drift signals, with $\ell_2$ regularization showing the most promising results. We observe that perturbation budget significantly influences the evasion-detectability trade-off, with increased budget leading to higher attack success rates and more substantial drift indicators.

</details>


### [127] [Provably Secure Steganography Based on List Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21394)
*Kaiyi Pang,Minhao Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于列表解码的可证明安全隐写方案，通过维护候选集合并引入后缀匹配机制，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了嵌入容量，理论证明其安全性与正确性，并给出了容量下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的可证明安全隐写方案嵌入容量不足，对LLM中固有的低熵特性适应性差，熵利用率低导致可行嵌入容量受限。

Method: 基于列表解码思想，维护包含正确秘密消息的候选集合而非直接寻找正确消息，从而充分利用生成文本的信息内容；引入后缀匹配机制从候选中区分正确消息；可即插即用，直接替换标准随机采样模块。

Result: 在三个LLM和七个PSS基线上的实验表明，该方法在计算效率与现有方案相当的同时，显著提升了嵌入容量。

Conclusion: 提出的方案在理论上证明了安全性和正确性，推导了理论容量下界，实际性能验证了其在LLM场景下实现高容量可证明安全隐写的有效性。

Abstract: Steganography embeds secret messages in seemingly innocuous carriers for covert communication under surveillance. Current Provably Secure Steganography (PSS) schemes based on language models can guarantee computational indistinguishability between the covertext and stegotext. However, achieving high embedding capacity remains a challenge for existing PSS. The inefficient entropy utilization renders them not well-suited for Large Language Models (LLMs), whose inherent low-entropy tendencies severely constrain feasible embedding capacity. To address this, we propose a provably secure steganography scheme with a theoretically proved high capacity. Our scheme is based on the concept of list decoding: it maintains a set of candidates that contain the correct secret message, instead of directly finding the correct message with more effort. This strategy fully utilizes the information content of the generated text, yielding higher capacity. To ensure the correctness of our scheme, we further introduce a suffix-matching mechanism to distinguish the correct secret message from the candidates. We provide theoretical proofs for both the security and correctness of our scheme, alongside a derivation of its theoretical capacity lower bound.
  Our approach is plug-and-play, requiring only a direct replacement of the model's standard random sampling module. Experiments on three LLMs and seven PSS baselines demonstrate that our method achieves computational efficiency comparable to prior PSS schemes while delivering a substantial improvement in embedding capacity.

</details>


### [128] [CSC: Turning the Adversary's Poison against Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21416)
*Yuchen Shi,Xin Guo,Huajie Chen,Tianqing Zhu,Bo Liu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cluster Segregation Concealment (CSC)方法，通过早期特征聚类分离中毒样本，并将其重新标记为虚拟类进行微调，在四个基准数据集上对抗十二种后门攻击，将平均攻击成功率降至接近零，同时保持模型干净精度几乎不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于毒药约束的防御方法对特定攻击变体的检测不足，且通过遗忘机制会损害模型效用导致精度下降。

Method: CSC分为两个阶段：(1) 隔离阶段：标准监督训练后，基于早期epoch的特征提取，使用DBSCAN聚类，根据类别多样性和密度指标识别异常簇；(2) 隐藏阶段：将识别出的中毒样本重新标记为虚拟类，通过交叉熵损失微调分类器，用良性虚拟关联替换后门关联。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上对抗十二种投毒攻击，CSC在平均攻击成功率方面优于九种最先进防御方法，将攻击成功率降至接近零，且干净精度损失最小。

Conclusion: CSC实现了鲁棒的后门模式识别、有效的隐藏机制以及卓越的实验验证，推动了可信人工智能的发展。

Abstract: Poisoning-based backdoor attacks pose significant threats to deep neural networks by embedding triggers in training data, causing models to misclassify triggered inputs as adversary-specified labels while maintaining performance on clean data. Existing poison restraint-based defenses often suffer from inadequate detection against specific attack variants and compromise model utility through unlearning methods that lead to accuracy degradation. This paper conducts a comprehensive analysis of backdoor attack dynamics during model training, revealing that poisoned samples form isolated clusters in latent space early on, with triggers acting as dominant features distinct from benign ones. Leveraging these insights, we propose Cluster Segregation Concealment (CSC), a novel poison suppression defense. CSC first trains a deep neural network via standard supervised learning while segregating poisoned samples through feature extraction from early epochs, DBSCAN clustering, and identification of anomalous clusters based on class diversity and density metrics. In the concealment stage, identified poisoned samples are relabeled to a virtual class, and the model's classifier is fine-tuned using cross-entropy loss to replace the backdoor association with a benign virtual linkage, preserving overall accuracy. CSC was evaluated on four benchmark datasets against twelve poisoning-based attacks, CSC outperforms nine state-of-the-art defenses by reducing average attack success rates to near zero with minimal clean accuracy loss. Contributions include robust backdoor patterns identification, an effective concealment mechanism, and superior empirical validation, advancing trustworthy artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [129] [Differentially Private De-identification of Dutch Clinical Notes: A Comparative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21421)
*Michele Miranda,Xinlan Yan,Nishant Mishra,Rachel Murphy,Ameen Abu-Hanna,Sébastien Bratières,Iacer Calixto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了差分隐私（DP）、命名实体识别（NER）和大型语言模型（LLM）三种方法在荷兰语临床文本去标识化中的表现，并探索了混合策略（NER/LLM预处理+DP）。结果表明，单独使用DP会严重降低数据效用，但与语言预处理（尤其是LLM）结合后，能显著改善隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保护患者隐私是临床文本二次利用的前提（遵守GDPR和HIPAA），但手工去标识化成本高昂且缓慢，亟需自动化方法在隐私保障与数据效用间取得平衡。

Method: 本文进行了DP、NER、LLM三种方法的对比实验，并设计了混合策略（先使用NER或LLM进行预处理，再施加DP），通过隐私泄露评估和外部评价（实体与关系分类）来衡量性能。

Result: 单独使用DP机制会严重降低数据效用；将DP与语言预处理（尤其是LLM）结合，能显著改善隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 混合策略（特别是LLM预处理+DP）比单一方法更优，为荷兰语临床文本去标识化提供了可行的隐私-效用平衡方案。

Abstract: Protecting patient privacy in clinical narratives is essential for enabling secondary use of healthcare data under regulations such as GDPR and HIPAA. While manual de-identification remains the gold standard, it is costly and slow, motivating the need for automated methods that combine privacy guarantees with high utility. Most automated text de-identification pipelines employed named entity recognition (NER) to identify protected entities for redaction. Although methods based on differential privacy (DP) provide formal privacy guarantees, more recently also large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for text de-identification in the clinical domain. In this work, we present the first comparative study of DP, NER, and LLMs for Dutch clinical text de-identification. We investigate these methods separately as well as hybrid strategies that apply NER or LLM preprocessing prior to DP, and assess performance in terms of privacy leakage and extrinsic evaluation (entity and relation classification). We show that DP mechanisms alone degrade utility substantially, but combining them with linguistic preprocessing, especially LLM-based redaction, significantly improves the privacy-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [130] [Benchmarking the Utility of Privacy-Preserving Cox Regression Under Data-Driven Clipping Bounds: A Multi-Dataset Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21491)
*Keita Fukuyama,Yukiko Mori,Tomohiro Kuroda,Hiroaki Kikuchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了差分隐私机制（拉普拉斯机制与随机响应）在Cox比例风险模型中对统计效用的影响。基于5个临床数据集、15个ε水平（0.1–1000）及1000次蒙特卡洛迭代，研究发现：在标准DP水平（ε ≤ 1）下，约90%的显著协变量失去显著性，预测性能接近随机水平。输出扰动（基于dfbeta的敏感性）在ε ≥ 5时表现接近基线；实际应用中，需要ε ≥ 10（预测性能）至ε ≥ 30–60（显著性保留）。数据驱动的裁剪边界（观测min/max）不提供正式ε-DP保证，结果代表正式DP下效用退化下限的乐观估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私被广泛认为是保护个体隐私的数学框架，但其对生存分析中统计效用的系统性影响尚缺乏全面评估。现有研究多聚焦于其他统计模型，缺少针对Cox比例风险模型在多种DP机制、数据集规模和隐私预算下效用的系统比较。

Method: 使用拉普拉斯机制和随机响应两种DP机制，结合数据驱动的裁剪边界（观测min/max）。比较三种输入扰动（仅协变量、所有输入、离散时间模型）和输出扰动（基于dfbeta的敏感性）方法。使用损失显著性率（LSR）、C指数和系数偏差作为评估指标，在5个临床数据集（n=168–6,524）和15个ε水平（0.1–1000）下进行1000次蒙特卡洛迭代。

Result: ε ≤ 1时约90%显著协变量失去显著性，预测性能接近随机水平。仅扰动协变量的输入扰动方法保留了风险集结构且恢复效果最佳；输出扰动在ε ≥ 5时接近基线性能。n ≈ 3,000时，显著性在ε=3–10快速恢复。实际需要ε ≥ 10（预测）至ε ≥ 30–60（显著性）。中等至高ε范围内，基线p值接近显著性阈值的变量出现假阳性率上升。

Conclusion: 在严格DP水平下，生存分析的统计效用严重受损；即使在大数据集（n=6,524）中也难以维持可用性。若要在保护隐私的同时保留合理的统计效用，需采用相对宽松的隐私预算（ε ≥ 10–60）。数据驱动的裁剪边界不提供形式化ε-DP保证，但可作为正式DP下效用退化的乐观下限参考。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) is a mathematical framework that guarantees individual privacy; however, systematic evaluation of its impact on statistical utility in survival analyses remains limited. In this study, we systematically evaluated the impact of DP mechanisms (Laplace mechanism and Randomized Response) with data-driven clipping bounds on the Cox proportional hazards model, using 5 clinical datasets ($n = 168$--$6{,}524$), 15 levels of $\varepsilon$ (0.1--1000), and $B = 1{,}000$ Monte Carlo iterations. The data-driven clipping bounds used here are observed min/max and therefore do not provide formal $\varepsilon$-DP guarantees; the results represent an optimistic lower bound on utility degradation under formal DP. We compared three types of input perturbations (covariates only, all inputs, and the discrete-time model) with output perturbations (dfbeta-based sensitivity), using loss of significance rate (LSR), C-index, and coefficient bias as metrics. At standard DP levels ($\varepsilon \leq 1$), approximately 90% (90--94%) of the significant covariates lost significance, even in the largest dataset ($n = 6{,}524$), and the predictive performance approached random levels (test C-index $\approx 0.5$) under many conditions. Among the input perturbation approaches, perturbing only covariates preserved the risk-set structure and achieved the best recovery, whereas output perturbation (dfbeta-based sensitivity) maintained near-baseline performance at $\varepsilon \geq 5$. At $n \approx 3{,}000$, the significance recovered rapidly at $\varepsilon = 3$--10; however, in practice, $\varepsilon \geq 10$ (for predictive performance) to $\varepsilon \geq 30$--60 (for significance preservation) is required. In the moderate-to-high $\varepsilon$ range, false-positive rates increased for variables whose baseline $p$-values were near the significance threshold.

</details>


### [131] [Stealthy Backdoor Attacks against LLMs Based on Natural Style Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21700)
*Jiali Wei,Ming Fan,Guoheng Sun,Xicheng Zhang,Haijun Wang,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BadStyle是一个完整的后门攻击框架，利用LLM生成带有不可察觉风格级触发器的自然隐蔽中毒样本，通过辅助目标损失稳定载荷注入，在多种LLM上实现了高攻击成功率和强隐蔽性，并能规避输入和输出级防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后门攻击方法存在三个主要缺陷：显式触发模式破坏自然性、长文本生成中攻击者指定载荷注入不稳定、威胁模型不完整导致后门传递和激活方式模糊。BadStyle旨在解决这些问题。

Method: BadStyle包括三部分：1) 利用LLM作为中毒样本生成器，构造携带隐式风格级触发器的自然隐蔽中毒样本；2) 设计辅助目标损失，在微调时强化中毒输入中目标内容的出现并惩罚其在良性响应中出现；3) 基于现实威胁模型，分别在提示注入和PEFT注入策略下进行评估。

Result: 在LLaMA、Phi、DeepSeek和GPT系列等七种受害者LLM上，BadStyle实现了高攻击成功率和强隐蔽性；辅助目标损失使风格级触发器的平均ASR提升约30%；后门在未知下游部署场景中仍然有效；能持续规避代表性输入级防御，并通过简单伪装绕过输出级防御。

Conclusion: BadStyle提供了一个完整的后门攻击框架，通过风格级触发器和辅助目标损失解决了现有方法的自然性、稳定性和威胁模型完整性问题，展示了LLM在安全关键领域部署中的严峻安全挑战。

Abstract: The growing application of large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical domains has raised urgent concerns about their security. Many recent studies have demonstrated the feasibility of backdoor attacks against LLMs. However, existing methods suffer from three key shortcomings: explicit trigger patterns that compromise naturalness, unreliable injection of attacker-specified payloads in long-form generation, and incompletely specified threat models that obscure how backdoors are delivered and activated in practice. To address these gaps, we present BadStyle, a complete backdoor attack framework and pipeline. BadStyle leverages an LLM as a poisoned sample generator to construct natural and stealthy poisoned samples that carry imperceptible style-level triggers while preserving semantics and fluency. To stabilize payload injection during fine-tuning, we design an auxiliary target loss that reinforces the attacker-specified target content in responses to poisoned inputs and penalizes its emergence in benign responses. We further ground the attack in a realistic threat model and systematically evaluate BadStyle under both prompt-induced and PEFT-based injection strategies. Extensive experiments across seven victim LLMs, including LLaMA, Phi, DeepSeek, and GPT series, demonstrate that BadStyle achieves high attack success rates (ASRs) while maintaining strong stealthiness. The proposed auxiliary target loss substantially improves the stability of backdoor activation, yielding an average ASR improvement of around 30% across style-level triggers. Even in downstream deployment scenarios unknown during injection, the implanted backdoor remains effective. Moreover, BadStyle consistently evades representative input-level defenses and bypasses output-level defenses through simple camouflage.

</details>


### [132] [Adversarial Robustness of Near-Field Millimeter-Wave Imaging under Waveform-Domain Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21774)
*Lhamo Dorje,Jordan Madden,Soamar Homsi,Xiaohua Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了近场毫米波成像在波形域物理攻击下的对抗鲁棒性，提出了一种利用可微成像流水线优化攻击波形的差分成像攻击框架，实验表明毫米波成像极易受到此类攻击，且深度学习算法比经典算法更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管毫米波成像已广泛应用于机场安检等安全关键场景，但其自身的安全性（尤其是对抗攻击下的鲁棒性）尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出了一种白盒对抗攻击模型和差分成像攻击框架，通过可微成像流水线优化攻击波形；并构建了包含干净波形和攻击波形的实测数据集。

Result: 在10种代表性成像算法上的实验表明，毫米波成像极易受到波形域物理攻击，攻击者能以中等发射功率隐藏或改变目标；令人意外的是，基于深度学习的成像算法比经典算法具有更高的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了毫米波成像系统存在严重的安全风险，亟需开发更鲁棒和安全的成像系统。

Abstract: Near-field millimeter-wave (mmWave) imaging is widely deployed in safety-critical applications such as airport passenger screening, yet its own security remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a systematic study of the adversarial robustness of mmWave imaging algorithms under waveform-domain physical attacks that directly manipulate the image reconstruction process. We propose a practical white-box adversarial model and develop a differential imaging attack framework that leverages the differentiable imaging pipeline to optimize attack waveforms. We also construct a real measured dataset of clean and attack waveforms using a mmWave imaging testbed. Experiments on 10 representative imaging algorithms show that mmWave imaging is highly vulnerable to such attacks, enabling an adversary to conceal or alter targets with moderate transmission power. Surprisingly, deep-learning-based imaging algorithms demonstrate higher robustness than classical algorithms. These findings expose critical security risks and motivate the development of robust and secure mmWave imaging systems.

</details>


### [133] [Black-Box Skill Stealing Attack from Proprietary LLM Agents: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21829)
*Zihan Wang,Rui Zhang,Yu Liu,Chi Liu,Qingchuan Zhao,Hongwei Li,Guowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究了针对LLM Agent系统的黑盒技能窃取攻击，发现仅需3次交互即可提取专有技能内容，并提出了跨输入、推理、输出三阶段的防御方案，但攻击成本低且易自动化，现有商业Agent生态系统普遍忽视了这一版权风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM Agent技能经济的兴起（已有超过9万个免费技能和20万个付费技能，创作者收入超10万美元），技能作为可复用的封装知识具有高商业价值。然而，攻击者可通过与公开Agent交互来提取隐藏的专有技能内容，目前这一安全威胁尚未被系统性研究。

Method: 首先从已有的提示窃取方法中推导出攻击分类法，然后构建自动化窃取提示生成Agent，流程包括：模型生成种子提示、通过场景合理化（scenario rationalization）和结构注入（structure injection）扩展提示、利用嵌入过滤（embedding filtering）确保多样性。最终形成可复现的评估流水线，在3种商业Agent架构和5种LLM上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，仅需3次交互即可成功提取Agent技能，构成严重的版权风险。防御方面，在输入、推理、输出三阶段设计的防御措施虽然取得了较好效果，但攻击成本低廉且可自动化，攻击者可通过多次尝试不同变体绕过防御，只需一次成功即可攻破受保护技能。

Conclusion: 当前专有Agent生态系统普遍忽视了技能窃取的版权风险，需要更鲁棒的防御策略来提供更强的保护保障。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on skills to encapsulate reusable capabilities via progressively disclosed instructions. High-quality skills inject expert knowledge into general-purpose models, improving performance on specialized tasks. This quality and ease of dissemination drive the emergence of a skill economy: free skill marketplaces already report 90368 published skills, while paid marketplaces report more than 2000 listings and over $100,000 in creator earnings. Yet this growing marketplace also creates a new attack surface, as adversaries can interact with public agent to extract hidden proprietary skill content. We present the first empirical study of black-box skill stealing against LLM agent systems. To study this threat, we first derive an attack taxonomy from prior prompt-stealing methods and build an automated stealing prompt generation agent. This agent starts from model-generated seed prompts, expands them through scenario rationalization and structure injection, and enforces diversity via embedding filtering. This process yields a reproducible pipeline for evaluating agent systems. We evaluate such attacks across 3 commercial agent architectures and 5 LLMs. Our results show that agent skills can be extracted with only 3 interactions, posing a serious copyright risk. To mitigate this threat, we design defenses across three stages of the agent pipeline: input, inference, and output. Although these defenses achieve strong results, the attack remains inexpensive and readily automatable, allowing an adversary to launch repeated attempts with different variants; only one successful attempt is sufficient to compromise the protected skill. Overall, our findings suggest that these copyright risks are largely overlooked across proprietary agent ecosystems. We therefore advocate for more robust defense strategies that provide stronger protection guarantees.

</details>


### [134] [Cross-Modal Phantom: Coordinated Camera-LiDAR Spoofing Against Multi-Sensor Fusion in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21841)
*Shahriar Rahman Khan,Raiful Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现自动驾驶多传感器融合（MSF）存在一种新型攻击：通过协同伪造跨传感器一致性（红外投影+LiDAR信号注入），使相机和激光雷达同时"看到"同一个虚假物体，从而绕过融合系统的冗余校验。在KITTI数据集上实现了85.5%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器融合（MSF）被设计为通过冗余感知来抵御单传感器失效，但融合过程本身是否存在新的安全漏洞尚不清楚。作者想要探究攻击者能否通过伪造跨传感器的一致性（使多个传感器对同一虚假目标达成共识）来绕过MSF的冗余防御机制。

Method: 设计了一种协调的、数据级（早期融合）攻击：1）模拟红外投影产生虚假相机检测结果；2）同步模拟LiDAR信号注入生成匹配的3D点簇。通过插入透视感知的图像patch和3D空间对齐的合成LiDAR点簇来模拟真实物理攻击的传感器级输出，在400个KITTI场景上进行评估。

Result: 在400个KITTI场景中，对最先进的感知模型实现了85.5%的成功攻击率。提供了首个定量证据表明恶意跨模态一致性可以攻破MSF感知系统。

Conclusion: 跨模态一致性攻击暴露了自动驾驶系统中核心数据融合逻辑的关键漏洞，MSF的冗余机制非但未能消除弱点，反而因融合逻辑自身可被欺骗而引入了新的攻击面。

Abstract: Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) increasingly depend on Multi-Sensor Fusion (MSF) to combine complementary modalities such as cameras and LiDAR for robust perception. While this redundancy is intended to safeguard against single-sensor failures, the fusion process itself introduces a subtle and underexplored vulnerability. In this work, we investigate whether an attacker can bypass MSF's redundancy by fabricating cross-sensor consistency, making multiple sensors agree on the same false object. We design a coordinated, data-level (early-fusion) attack that emulates the outcome of two synchronized physical spoofing sources: an infrared (IR) projection that induces a false camera detection and a LiDAR signal injection that produces a matching 3D point cluster. Rather than implementing the physical attack hardware, we simulate its sensor-level outcomes by inserting perspective-aware image patches and synthetic LiDAR point clusters aligned in 3D space. This approach preserves the perceptual effects that real IR and IEMI-based spoofing would create at the sensor output. Using 400 KITTI scenes, our large-scale evaluation shows that the coordinated spoofing deceives a state-of-the-art perception model with an 85.5% successful attack rate. These findings provide the first quantitative evidence that malicious cross-modal consistency can compromise MSF-based perception, revealing a critical vulnerability in the core data-fusion logic of modern autonomous vehicle systems.

</details>


### [135] [Transient Turn Injection: Exposing Stateless Multi-Turn Vulnerabilities in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21860)
*Naheed Rayhan,Sohely Jahan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为瞬态轮次注入（TTI）的新型多轮攻击技术，利用无状态审核机制的漏洞，通过将对抗意图分布到多个独立交互中来规避策略执行。在包括OpenAI、Anthropic、Google Gemini、Meta及多种开源模型上的评估揭示了不同模型对TTI攻击的鲁棒性差异显著，并发现特定于模型的漏洞，尤其是在医疗等高危领域。研究还提出了会话级上下文聚合等缓解策略，强调了持续对抗性测试的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被集成到敏感工作流中，其对抗鲁棒性和安全性变得至关重要。现有越狱攻击通常依赖持续的对话上下文，而本文旨在利用无状态审核机制的固有缺陷，提出一种无需维持持久上下文的多轮攻击方法，以揭示当前安全防护的盲区。

Method: TTI攻击技术通过LLM驱动的自动化攻击智能体，将恶意意图分解并分布到多个独立交互轮次中，从而绕过单轮次的内容审核。研究采用自动化黑盒评估框架，在多个商用和开源LLM上进行系统测试，并与既有对抗提示方法进行对比。

Result: 不同模型对TTI攻击的鲁棒性存在显著差异，仅少数架构表现出本质上的鲁棒性。在医疗等高危领域发现了此前未知的模型特定漏洞和攻击面模式。TTI相比传统越狱方法展现出更高的规避成功率。

Conclusion: 研究强调了需要整体性的、上下文感知的防御机制以及持续的对抗性测试，以应对不断演变的多轮威胁。会话级上下文聚合和深度对齐是有效的缓解方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into sensitive workflows, raising the stakes for adversarial robustness and safety. This paper introduces Transient Turn Injection(TTI), a new multi-turn attack technique that systematically exploits stateless moderation by distributing adversarial intent across isolated interactions. TTI leverages automated attacker agents powered by large language models to iteratively test and evade policy enforcement in both commercial and open-source LLMs, marking a departure from conventional jailbreak approaches that typically depend on maintaining persistent conversational context. Our extensive evaluation across state-of-the-art models-including those from OpenAI, Anthropic, Google Gemini, Meta, and prominent open-source alternatives-uncovers significant variations in resilience to TTI attacks, with only select architectures exhibiting substantial inherent robustness. Our automated blackbox evaluation framework also uncovers previously unknown model specific vulnerabilities and attack surface patterns, especially within medical and high stakes domains. We further compare TTI against established adversarial prompting methods and detail practical mitigation strategies, such as session level context aggregation and deep alignment approaches. Our study underscores the urgent need for holistic, context aware defenses and continuous adversarial testing to future proof LLM deployments against evolving multi-turn threats.

</details>


### [136] [CrossCommitVuln-Bench: A Dataset of Multi-Commit Python Vulnerabilities Invisible to Per-Commit Static Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21917)
*Arunabh Majumdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了CrossCommitVuln-Bench基准，包含15个真实Python漏洞(CVE)，每个漏洞的利用条件跨多次提交引入。评估显示：按提交检测率(CCDR)仅13%，累积模式也仅27%，表明跨提交漏洞对传统SAST工具几乎不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态分析工具(SAST)在单次提交级别检测漏洞，但许多真实漏洞的利用条件是跨多个提交逐步引入的，每个提交单独看是良性的。目前缺乏专门针对这类跨提交漏洞的基准测试集，导致该问题未得到充分研究和量化。

Method: 从15个真实Python CVE出发，人工标注每个漏洞的贡献提交链、每个提交逃避单次分析的结构化理由；使用Semgrep和Bandit在两种模式(按提交扫描和累积扫描)下进行基线评估。

Result: 按提交检测率(CCDR)仅13%，87%的漏洞链对按提交SAST不可见。两个被检测到的案例质量也很差：一个出现在安全修复提交上(开发者会抑制告警)，另一个只检测到次要的硬编码密钥组件而完全忽略了主要漏洞(200+未保护API端点)。累积模式检测率也仅27%。

Conclusion: 当前基于快照的SAST工具在检测跨提交引入的漏洞方面表现严重不足，需要新的跨提交漏洞检测方法。数据集和工具已开源以支持该方向的研究。

Abstract: We present CrossCommitVuln-Bench, a curated benchmark of 15 real-world Python vulnerabilities (CVEs) in which the exploitable condition was introduced across multiple commits - each individually benign to per-commit static analysis - but collectively critical. We manually annotate each CVE with its contributing commit chain, a structured rationale for why each commit evades per-commit analysis, and baseline evaluations using Semgrep and Bandit in both per-commit and cumulative scanning modes. Our central finding: the per-commit detection rate (CCDR) is 13% across all 15 vulnerabilities - 87% of chains are invisible to per-commit SAST. Critically, both per-commit detections are qualitatively poor: one occurs on commits framed as security fixes (where developers suppress the alert), and the other detects only the minor hardcoded-key component while completely missing the primary vulnerability (200+ unprotected API endpoints). Even in cumulative mode (full codebase present), the detection rate is only 27%, confirming that snapshot-based SAST tools often miss vulnerabilities whose introduction spans multiple commits. The dataset, annotation schema, evaluation scripts, and reproducible baselines are released under open-source licenses to support research on cross-commit vulnerability detection.

</details>


### [137] [Behavioral Consistency and Transparency Analysis on Large Language Model API Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21083)
*Guanjie Lin,Yinxin Wan,Shichao Pei,Ting Xu,Kuai Xu,Guoliang Xue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GateScope，一个轻量级黑盒测量框架，用于评估商业LLM API网关的行为一致性和操作透明度。通过对10个真实网关的测量，发现存在模型无声替换、记忆退化、定价偏差和延迟不稳定等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方LLM API网关作为多模型统一接入点日益流行，但其内部路由、缓存和计费策略不透明，用户无法验证广告模型是否被实际使用、响应是否忠实于上游API、账单是否准确，存在信任缺口。

Method: GateScope从四个维度审计网关：响应内容分析、多轮对话性能、计费准确性和延迟特征，通过黑盒方式检测模型降级/切换、静默截断、计费偏差和延迟不稳定等违规行为。

Result: 在10个商业LLM API网关上的测量揭示了预期行为与实际行为之间的频繁差距，包括无声模型替换、记忆保持能力下降、偏离公开定价以及不同平台间延迟稳定性的显著差异。

Conclusion: 当前商业LLM API网关在行为一致性和操作透明性方面存在普遍问题，GateScope框架能有效检测这些违规行为，为用户提供可见性，并呼吁网关提供商提升透明度。

Abstract: Third-party Large Language Model (LLM) API gateways are rapidly emerging as unified access points to models offered by multiple vendors. However, the internal routing, caching, and billing policies of these gateways are largely undisclosed, leaving users with limited visibility into whether requests are served by the advertised models, whether responses remain faithful to upstream APIs, or whether invoices accurately reflect public pricing policies. To address this gap, we introduce GateScope, a lightweight black-box measurement framework for evaluating behavioral consistency and operational transparency in commercial LLM gateways. GateScope is designed to detect key misbehaviors, including model downgrading or switching, silent truncation, billing inaccuracies, and instability in latency by auditing gateways along four critical dimensions: response content analysis, multi-turn conversation performance, billing accuracy, and latency characteristics. Our measurements across 10 real-world commercial LLM API gateways reveal frequent gaps between expected and actual behaviors, including silent model substitutions, degraded memory retention, deviations from announced pricing, and substantial variation in latency stability across platforms.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Achieving the Kesten-Stigum bound in the non-uniform hypergraph stochastic block model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20907)
*Manuel Fernandez,Ludovic Stephan,Yizhe Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究非均匀超图随机块模型（HSBM）中的社区检测问题，其中超边大小各异。针对r=2块的情况，证明了当所有均匀超图层面的信噪比之和超过1时，弱恢复是可能的，从而确认了Chodrow等人（2023）猜想中的正向部分。同时，通过最优加权的非回溯算子提出了一个达到此阈值的多项式时间谱算法，并发展了加权非均匀超图上非回溯算子的谱理论，包括新颖的Ihara-Bass公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非均匀超图随机块模型（HSBM）中，多种大小的超边共存，捕捉了高阶和多视角交互。核心问题是：多个低于检测阈值的均匀超图层面能否通过组合实现弱恢复？该问题对于理解异质高阶交互的聚合机制至关重要。

Method: 建立了弱恢复的Kesten-Stigum型界；发展了加权非均匀超图上非回溯算子的谱理论，包括特征值异常值和特征向量重叠的精确刻画；提出了新颖的适用于加权非均匀超图的Ihara-Bass公式，实现高效低维表示和可证明的谱重建算法。

Result: 对于r=2块，证明了当所有均匀超图层的信噪比之和超过1时弱恢复是可能的，确认了Chodrow等人（2023）的正向猜想；提供了达到此阈值的最优加权非回溯算子的多项式时间谱算法；未加权非回溯矩阵的谱方法达到了另一个算法阈值，同样对应于Chodrow等人的猜想。

Conclusion: 这些结果为非均匀超图聚类提供了原则性且计算高效的方法，突显了最优加权在聚合异构高阶交互中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study the community detection problem in the non-uniform hypergraph stochastic block model (HSBM), where hyperedges of varying sizes coexist. This setting captures higher-order and multi-view interactions and raises a fundamental question: can multiple uniform hypergraph layers below the detection threshold be combined to enable weak recovery? We answer this question by establishing a Kesten--Stigum-type bound for weak recovery in a general class of non-uniform HSBMs with $r$ blocks, generated according to multiple symmetric probability tensors. In the case $r=2$, we show that weak recovery is possible whenever the sum of the signal-to-noise ratios across all uniform hypergraph layers exceeds one, thereby confirming the positive part of a conjecture in (Chodrow et al., 2023). Moreover, we provide a polynomial-time spectral algorithm that achieves this threshold via an optimally weighted non-backtracking operator. For the unweighted non-backtracking matrix, our spectral method attains a different algorithmic threshold, also conjectured in (Chodrow et al., 2023).
  Our approach develops a spectral theory for weighted non-backtracking operators on non-uniform hypergraphs, including a precise characterization of outlier eigenvalues and eigenvector overlaps. We introduce a novel Ihara--Bass formula tailored to weighted non-uniform hypergraphs, which yields an efficient low-dimensional representation and leads to a provable spectral reconstruction algorithm. Taken together, these results provide a principled and computationally efficient approach to clustering in non-uniform hypergraphs, and highlight the role of optimal weighting in aggregating heterogeneous higher-order interactions.

</details>


### [139] [Learning to Emulate Chaos: Adversarial Optimal Transport Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21097)
*Gabriel Melo,Leonardo Santiago,Peter Y. Lu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于对抗最优传输的目标函数（Sinkhorn散度/2-Wasserstein和WGAN对偶形式/1-Wasserstein）来联合学习高质量统计摘要和物理一致的混沌系统仿真器，实验证明该方法能显著提升长期统计保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌系统对初始条件高度敏感，传统平方误差损失在噪声数据上训练时难以实现长期预测，而现有方法依赖手工设计的局部特征和统计摘要，缺乏自动学习高质量统计量的机制。

Method: 提出对抗最优传输目标函数族，包括基于Sinkhorn散度的2-Wasserstein距离和基于WGAN对偶形式的1-Wasserstein距离，在训练仿真器的同时联合学习统计摘要。

Result: 在多种混沌系统（包括高维混沌吸引子）上，采用该方法训练的仿真器在长期统计保真度上显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 对抗最优传输方法能有效提升混沌系统数据驱动仿真器的长期统计建模能力，且具有理论保证和实验验证。

Abstract: Chaos arises in many complex dynamical systems, from weather to power grids, but is difficult to accurately model using data-driven emulators, including neural operator architectures. For chaotic systems, the inherent sensitivity to initial conditions makes exact long-term forecasts theoretically infeasible, meaning that traditional squared-error losses often fail when trained on noisy data. Recent work has focused on training emulators to match the statistical properties of chaotic attractors by introducing regularization based on handcrafted local features and summary statistics, as well as learned statistics extracted from a diverse dataset of trajectories. In this work, we propose a family of adversarial optimal transport objectives that jointly learn high-quality summary statistics and a physically consistent emulator. We theoretically analyze and experimentally validate a Sinkhorn divergence formulation (2-Wasserstein) and a WGAN-style dual formulation (1-Wasserstein). Our experiments across a variety of chaotic systems, including systems with high-dimensional chaotic attractors, show that emulators trained with our approach exhibit significantly improved long-term statistical fidelity.

</details>


### [140] [Refining Covariance Matrix Estimation in Stochastic Gradient Descent Through Bias Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21203)
*Ziyang Wei,Wanrong Zhu,Jingyang Lyu,Wei Biao Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需二阶导数信息的全在线去偏协方差估计器，用于随机梯度下降(SGD)算法的在线推断和渐近协方差估计，该估计器在收敛速度上优于现有无Hessian方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SGD的在线推断方法（如插值法和批均值法）要么需要难以获取的二阶Hessian信息，要么存在收敛速度慢的问题，这限制了SGD算法在实际应用中的统计推断能力。

Method: 提出了一种全在线去偏协方差估计器，通过偏置减少技术实现，无需二阶导数信息，显著提高了估计精度。

Result: 该方法实现了$n^{(α-1)/2} \sqrt{\log n}$的收敛速度，优于现有的无Hessian方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的全在线去偏协方差估计器在无需二阶导数信息的前提下，有效提升了SGD算法在线推断的精度和收敛速度，为大规模机器学习中的统计推断提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: We study online inference and asymptotic covariance estimation for the stochastic gradient descent (SGD) algorithm. While classical methods (such as plug-in and batch-means estimators) are available, they either require inaccessible second-order (Hessian) information or suffer from slow convergence. To address these challenges, we propose a novel, fully online de-biased covariance estimator that eliminates the need for second-order derivatives while significantly improving estimation accuracy. Our method employs a bias-reduction technique to achieve a convergence rate of $n^{(α-1)/2} \sqrt{\log n}$, outperforming existing Hessian-free alternatives.

</details>


### [141] [Calibeating Prediction-Powered Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21260)
*Lars van der Laan,Mark Van Der Laan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出校准预测驱动推断（Calibrated PPI），通过对黑箱预测模型进行事后校准（线性和保序回归），提升半监督均值估计的效率和鲁棒性，理论证明了保序校准的一阶最优性，实验表明该方法优于PPI且与AIPW、PPI++竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督均值估计中，AIPW方法对预测模型误设定具有鲁棒性，但当预测分数与结果变量尺度不匹配时效率低下。现有PPI方法在该场景下可能因校准不足而表现不佳。因此需要一种能灵活校准预测分数以提升估计效率的方法。

Method: 在标记样本上对黑箱预测模型进行事后校准（线性校准或保序校准），将校准后的预测分数用于半监督估计。线性校准等价于PPI++，保序校准具有一阶最优性。理论分析了现有PPI、PPI++与AIPW的关系，证明PPI是AIPW的特例，PPI++是经验效率最大化的AIPW。

Result: 保序校准提升预测精度和估计效率，且任何进一步的保序后处理无法获得额外的一阶增益。仿真和真实数据实验中，校准估计器通常优于PPI，与AIPW和PPI++竞争或更优。

Conclusion: 事后校准是一种简单有效的方法，无需重新训练模型即可显著提升半监督推断的效率，为预测驱动推断提供了一种实用且理论保障的改进方案。

Abstract: We study semisupervised mean estimation with a small labeled sample, a large unlabeled sample, and a black-box prediction model whose output may be miscalibrated. A standard approach in this setting is augmented inverse-probability weighting (AIPW) [Robins et al., 1994], which protects against prediction-model misspecification but can be inefficient when the prediction score is poorly aligned with the outcome scale. We introduce Calibrated Prediction-Powered Inference, which post-hoc calibrates the prediction score on the labeled sample before using it for semisupervised estimation. This simple step requires no retraining and can improve the original score both as a predictor of the outcome and as a regression adjustment for semisupervised inference. We study both linear and isotonic calibration. For isotonic calibration, we establish first-order optimality guarantees: isotonic post-processing can improve predictive accuracy and estimator efficiency relative to the original score and simpler post-processing rules, while no further post-processing of the fitted isotonic score yields additional first-order gains. For linear calibration, we show first-order equivalence to PPI++. We also clarify the relationship among existing estimators, showing that the original PPI estimator is a special case of AIPW and can be inefficient when the prediction model is accurate, while PPI++ is AIPW with empirical efficiency maximization [Rubin et al., 2008]. In simulations and real-data experiments, our calibrated estimators often outperform PPI and are competitive with, or outperform, AIPW and PPI++. We provide an accompanying Python package, ppi_aipw, at https://larsvanderlaan.github.io/ppi-aipw/.

</details>


### [142] [CLT-Optimal Parameter Error Bounds for Linear System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21270)
*Yichen Zhou,Stephen Tu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文指出现有的线性动力系统（LDS）参数估计有限样本界不够精确，在状态维度上高估了误差；通过二阶参数误差分解（包含矩阵值鞅项）提出了更紧的界，在Frobenius范数下达到实例最优常数倍，在谱范数下仅多出状态维度的多对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前系统辨识的非渐近有限样本界虽然在过去十年取得了显著进展，但在最基本的离散时间线性动力系统（LDS）的普通最小二乘（OLS）估计中，这些最先进的界并不能准确捕捉统计复杂度，在谱范数和Frobenius范数下均高估了状态维度量级的参数误差。

Method: 利用渐近正态性识别出当前界高估误差的问题实例类别；提出一种新颖的二阶参数误差分解方法，其中低阶项是一个矩阵值鞅，能够正确捕捉中心极限定理（CLT）的缩放行为。

Result: 对于（i）稳定系统和（ii）多轨迹设置，在Frobenius范数下达到了实例最优常数倍的有界，在谱范数下仅多出状态维度的多对数因子，显著优于现有界。

Conclusion: 通过更精细的二阶分解，弥合了现有非渐近界与真实统计复杂性之间的差距，为LDS参数估计提供了理论上更紧且实践上更有指导意义的有限样本保证。

Abstract: There has been remarkable progress over the past decade in establishing finite-sample, non-asymptotic bounds on recovering unknown system parameters from observed system behavior. Surprisingly, however, we show that the current state-of-the-art bounds do not accurately capture the statistical complexity of system identification, even in the most fundamental setting of estimating a discrete-time linear dynamical system (LDS) via ordinary least-squares regression (OLS). Specifically, we utilize asymptotic normality to identify classes of problem instances for which current bounds overstate the squared parameter error, in both spectral and Frobenius norm, by a factor of the state-dimension of the system. Informed by this discrepancy, we then sharpen the OLS parameter error bounds via a novel second-order decomposition of the parameter error, where crucially the lower-order term is a matrix-valued martingale that we show correctly captures the CLT scaling. From our analysis we obtain finite-sample bounds for both (i) stable systems and (ii) the many-trajectories setting that match the instance-specific optimal rates up to constant factors in Frobenius norm, and polylogarithmic state-dimension factors in spectral norm.

</details>


### [143] [A single algorithm for both restless and rested rotting bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21432)
*Julien Seznec,Pierre Ménard,Alessandro Lazaric,Michal Valko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出RAW-UCB算法，能在无需先验知识的情况下，在腐烂的rested和restless bandit两种设定中均实现近乎最优的遗憾度，解决了此前认为这两种问题本质不同的认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用场景中动作的奖励随时间衰减（如用户对重复推荐感到厌倦、内容过时），这些现象可建模为rested和restless bandit中的腐烂问题。此前Levine等人(2017)表明这两种设定差异显著，现有算法无法同时处理。本文旨在设计一种无需先验知识、在两种设定下均表现良好的算法。

Method: 提出Rotting Adaptive Window UCB (RAW-UCB)算法，该算法采用自适应窗口机制，能够在不事先知道设定类型（rested或restless）和非平稳性类型（如分段常数、有界变差）的情况下，同时处理两种腐烂bandit问题。

Result: RAW-UCB在rested和restless腐烂bandit设定中均达到近乎最优的遗憾度，与此前认为奖励允许增加时没有算法能达成类似结果的负面结论形成鲜明对比。在合成数据和基于真实数据集的实验中验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 本文证明腐烂bandit问题（rested和restless）可以被统一算法有效解决，打破了此前认为两者本质不同的认知壁垒。RAW-UCB无需任何先验知识即可自适应地对两种非平稳设定取得近乎最优性能。

Abstract: In many application domains (e.g., recommender systems, intelligent tutoring systems), the rewards associated to the actions tend to decrease over time. This decay is either caused by the actions executed in the past (e.g., a user may get bored when songs of the same genre are recommended over and over) or by an external factor (e.g., content becomes outdated). These two situations can be modeled as specific instances of the rested and restless bandit settings, where arms are rotting (i.e., their value decrease over time). These problems were thought to be significantly different, since Levine et al. (2017) showed that state-of-the-art algorithms for restless bandit perform poorly in the rested rotting setting. In this paper, we introduce a novel algorithm, Rotting Adaptive Window UCB (RAW-UCB), that achieves near-optimal regret in both rotting rested and restless bandit, without any prior knowledge of the setting (rested or restless) and the type of non-stationarity (e.g., piece-wise constant, bounded variation). This is in striking contrast with previous negative results showing that no algorithm can achieve similar results as soon as rewards are allowed to increase. We confirm our theoretical findings on a number of synthetic and dataset-based experiments.

</details>


### [144] [A Kernel Nonconformity Score for Multivariate Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21595)
*Louis Meyer,Wenkai Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出多变量核分(MKS)方法，通过将残差向量压缩为标量同时保留几何结构，实现自适应预测区域，具备有限样本覆盖保证和维度无关的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量共形预测需要能将残差向量压缩为标量的非一致性分数，同时保留残差分布的隐式几何结构。现有方法未能显式适应几何结构，导致预测区域体积过大。

Method: 提出多变量核分数(MKS)，形似高斯过程后验方差，可分解为各向异性的最大均值差异(MMD)，在核密度估计与协方差加权距离之间插值。利用核协方差算子的有效秩而非环境维度建立收敛速率。

Result: 相比椭球基线方法，MKS在保持名义覆盖率的条件下显著减小预测区域体积，维度越高、覆盖率越严格时增益越大。

Conclusion: MKS统一了贝叶斯不确定性量化与频率学派覆盖保证，实现了维度无关的自适应多变量共形预测。

Abstract: Multivariate conformal prediction requires nonconformity scores that compress residual vectors into scalars while preserving certain implicit geometric structure of the residual distribution. We introduce a Multivariate Kernel Score (MKS) that produces prediction regions that explicitly adapt to this geometry. We show that the proposed score resembles the Gaussian process posterior variance, unifying Bayesian uncertainty quantification with the coverage guarantees of frequentist-type. Moreover, the MKS can be decomposed into an anisotropic Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) that interpolates between kernel density estimation and covariance-weighted distance.
