We prove finite-sample coverage guarantees and establish convergence rates that depend on the effective rank of the kernel-based covariance operator rather than the ambient dimension, enabling dimension-free adaptation. On regression tasks, the MKS reduces the volume of prediction region significantly, compared to ellipsoidal baselines while maintaining nominal coverage, with larger gains at higher dimensions and tighter coverage levels.

</details>


### [145] [There Will Be a Scientific Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21691)
*Jamie Simon,Daniel Kunin,Alexander Atanasov,Enric Boix-Adserà,Blake Bordelon,Jeremy Cohen,Nikhil Ghosh,Florentin Guth,Arthur Jacot,Mason Kamb,Dhruva Karkada,Eric J. Michaud,Berkan Ottlik,Joseph Turnbull*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出深度学习科学理论正在形成，称之为"学习力学"（learning mechanics），该理论关注训练过程的动力学、粗粒度统计量和可证伪的定量预测，并总结了五个关键研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证一个统一的深度学习科学理论正在浮现，并为该理论命名和定义其核心特征，同时澄清其与统计视角、信息论视角及机械可解释性之间的关系。

Method: 通过梳理和整合当前深度学习理论的五大研究主线：(a) 可解的理想化设定、(b) 可处理极限、(c) 简单数学定律、(d) 超参数理论、(e) 普适行为，并总结它们的共同特征。

Result: 识别出五类指向该理论的研究工作；发现这些工作共同关注训练动力学、粗粒度聚合统计量和可证伪的定量预测；提出"学习力学"这一命名。

Conclusion: 学习力学将有助于理解深度学习的学习过程，与机械可解释性存在共生关系；指出了重要的开放方向和对初学者的建议。

Abstract: In this paper, we make the case that a scientific theory of deep learning is emerging. By this we mean a theory which characterizes important properties and statistics of the training process, hidden representations, final weights, and performance of neural networks. We pull together major strands of ongoing research in deep learning theory and identify five growing bodies of work that point toward such a theory: (a) solvable idealized settings that provide intuition for learning dynamics in realistic systems; (b) tractable limits that reveal insights into fundamental learning phenomena; (c) simple mathematical laws that capture important macroscopic observables; (d) theories of hyperparameters that disentangle them from the rest of the training process, leaving simpler systems behind; and (e) universal behaviors shared across systems and settings which clarify which phenomena call for explanation.
  Taken together, these bodies of work share certain broad traits: they are concerned with the dynamics of the training process; they primarily seek to describe coarse aggregate statistics; and they emphasize falsifiable quantitative predictions. We argue that the emerging theory is best thought of as a mechanics of the learning process, and suggest the name learning mechanics. We discuss the relationship between this mechanics perspective and other approaches for building a theory of deep learning, including the statistical and information-theoretic perspectives. In particular, we anticipate a symbiotic relationship between learning mechanics and mechanistic interpretability.
  We also review and address common arguments that fundamental theory will not be possible or is not important. We conclude with a portrait of important open directions in learning mechanics and advice for beginners. We host further introductory materials, perspectives, and open questions at learningmechanics.pub.

</details>


### [146] [Beyond Expected Information Gain: Stable Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design with Integral Probability Metrics and Plug-and-Play Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21849)
*Di Wu,Ling Liang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于积分概率度量（IPM）的贝叶斯最优实验设计（BOED）框架，替代传统基于KL散度的期望信息增益（EIG）方法，解决支持不匹配、尾部低估和罕见事件敏感性等根本问题，并通过理论保证和实验验证了其在几何感知稳定性、高维设计等方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BOED依赖KL散度定义的EIG，存在嵌套期望计算困难、支持不匹配、尾部低估和罕见事件敏感性等固有问题，制约了其在资源受限场景下的应用效果。

Method: 提出IPM-BOED框架，用Wasserstein距离、最大均值差异（MMD）和能量距离等积分概率度量替代密度散度，构建即插即用的BOED框架；并进一步扩展至神经最优传输估计器等几何感知度量的样本化BOED模板。

Result: 理论证明IPM效用函数在替代模型误差和先验误指定下比经典EIG效用具有更强的几何感知稳定性；实验表明IPM设计能产生高度集中的可信集，并在高维场景下优于传统嵌套蒙特卡洛和先进变分方法。

Conclusion: IPM-BOED框架有效解决了KL散度BOED的核心瓶颈，提供了更灵活、稳定且适用于高维场景的实验设计方案，具有良好的理论支撑和实证效果。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design (BOED) provides a rigorous framework for decision-making tasks in which data acquisition is often the critical bottleneck, especially in resource-constrained settings. Traditionally, BOED typically selects designs by maximizing expected information gain (EIG), commonly defined through the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence. However, classical evaluation of EIG often involves challenging nested expectations, and even advanced variational methods leave the underlying log-density-ratio objective unchanged. As a result, support mismatch, tail underestimation, and rare-event sensitivity remain intrinsic concerns for KL-based BOED. To address these fundamental bottlenecks, we introduce an IPM-based BOED framework that replaces density-based divergences with integral probability metrics (IPMs), including the Wasserstein distance, Maximum Mean Discrepancy, and Energy Distance, resulting in a highly flexible plug-and-play BOED framework. We establish theoretical guarantees showing that IPM-based utilities provide stronger geometry-aware stability under surrogate-model error and prior misspecification than classical EIG-based utilities. We also validate the proposed framework empirically, demonstrating that IPM-based designs yield highly concentrated credible sets. Furthermore, by extending the same sample-based BOED template in a plug-and-play manner to geometry-aware discrepancies beyond the IPM class, illustrated by a neural optimal transport estimator, we achieve accurate optimal designs in high-dimensional settings where conventional nested Monte Carlo estimators and advanced variational methods fail.

</details>


### [147] [Revealing Geography-Driven Signals in Zone-Level Claim Frequency Models: An Empirical Study using Environmental and Visual Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21893)
*Sherly Alfonso-Sánchez,Cristián Bravo,Kristina G. Stankova*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究利用公开数据集BeMTPL97，在区域级建模框架下，通过环境指标（OpenStreetMap、CORINE土地覆盖）和遥感影像（比利时国家地理研究所）两种渠道引入地理信息，评估其对MTPL索赔频率预测的贡献。结果表明，地理信息能提升模型精度，环境特征比影像嵌入更具价值，地理信息的预测效果更依赖于表征方式而非模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算数据集中位置标识符有限，限制了地理背景信息在索赔频率模型中的应用。本研究旨在探讨在个体级空间信息受限的情况下，如何从替代数据源（开放地图、土地覆盖、遥感影像）中提取地理信息并纳入精算模型，以提高MTPL索赔预测的准确性。

Method: 采用区域级建模框架，基于BeMTPL97数据集，在未见过邮政编码上评估预测性能。地理信息通过两个渠道引入：环境指标（OpenStreetMap和CORINE土地覆盖）以及比利时国家地理研究所的遥感影像。使用GLM、正则化GLM和梯度提升树三种基线模型评估坐标、环境特征和影像嵌入的预测贡献，原始影像则使用CNN建模。

Result: 用构建的地理信息增强精算变量可提升预测精度。线性和树模型均从5公里尺度的坐标与环境特征组合中获益最多。影像嵌入在已有环境特征时无额外提升，但缺乏环境特征时，预训练视觉Transformer嵌入可提高正则化GLM的精度和稳定性。地理信息的预测价值更取决于表征方式而非模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 地理信息能够提升区域级MTPL索赔频率模型的预测性能，其效果依赖于如何表征地理信息；即使在个体级空间信息有限的约束下，仍可通过替代数据源有效引入地理背景信息。

Abstract: Geographic context is often consider relevant to motor insurance risk, yet public actuarial datasets provide limited location identifiers, constraining how this information can be incorporated and evaluated in claim-frequency models. This study examines how geographic information from alternative data sources can be incorporated into actuarial models for Motor Third Party Liability (MTPL) claim prediction under such constraints.
  Using the BeMTPL97 dataset, we adopt a zone-level modeling framework and evaluate predictive performance on unseen postcodes. Geographic information is introduced through two channels: environmental indicators from OpenStreetMap and CORINE Land Cover, and orthoimagery released by the Belgian National Geographic Institute for academic use. We evaluate the predictive contribution of coordinates, environmental features, and image embeddings across three baseline models: generalized linear models (GLMs), regularized GLMs, and gradient-boosted trees, while raw imagery is modeled using convolutional neural networks.
  Our results show that augmenting actuarial variables with constructed geographic information improves accuracy. Across experiments, both linear and tree-based models benefit most from combining coordinates with environmental features extracted at 5 km scale, while smaller neighborhoods also improve baseline specifications. Generally, image embeddings do not improve performance when environmental features are available; however, when such features are absent, pretrained vision-transformer embeddings enhance accuracy and stability for regularized GLMs. Our results show that the predictive value of geographic information in zone-level MTPL frequency models depends less on model complexity than on how geography is represented, and illustrate that geographic context can be incorporated despite limited individual-level spatial information.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [The Sample Complexity of Multicalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21923)
*Natalie Collina,Jiuyao Lu,Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了批量设置下多校准（multicalibration）的极小化样本复杂度，证明了对于固定κ>0且|G|≤ε^{-κ}时，样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-3})（上下界匹配），并将其与边际校准的Θ̃(ε^{-2})分离开来。更一般地，对于1≤p≤2的加权L_p多校准度量，建立了最优指数为3/p的匹配上下界，并将下界模板推广到一类可elicitable性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多校准在批量学习中的样本复杂度，并将其与边际校准、在线设置下的复杂度进行对比，揭示不同校准任务之间的本质差异。

Method: 通过在线到批量的归约（online-to-batch reduction）构造随机预测器实现上界；通过信息论下界技术证明样本复杂度的下界，并将下界模板推广至更广泛的elicitable性质族。

Result: 1) 当|G|≤ε^{-κ}（κ>0）时，多校准的样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-3})；2) 当κ=0时，样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-2})，呈现尖锐阈值现象；3) 对于加权L_p度量（1≤p≤2），最优指数为3/p；4) 下界模板可推广至expectiles和有界密度分位数等性质。

Conclusion: 批量设置下的均值ECE多校准与在线设置下同样困难（均需Θ̃(ε^{-3})样本），而边际校准在批量下更容易（Θ̃(ε^{-2})），说明多校准与边际校准有本质不同的样本复杂度特征。

Abstract: We study the minimax sample complexity of multicalibration in the batch setting. A learner observes $n$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution and must output a (possibly randomized) predictor whose population multicalibration error, measured by Expected Calibration Error (ECE), is at most $\varepsilon$ with respect to a given family of groups. For every fixed $κ> 0$, in the regime $|G|\le \varepsilon^{-κ}$, we prove that $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-3})$ samples are necessary and sufficient, up to polylogarithmic factors. The lower bound holds even for randomized predictors, and the upper bound is realized by a randomized predictor obtained via an online-to-batch reduction. This separates the sample complexity of multicalibration from that of marginal calibration, which scales as $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$, and shows that mean-ECE multicalibration is as difficult in the batch setting as it is in the online setting, in contrast to marginal calibration which is strictly more difficult in the online setting. In contrast we observe that for $κ= 0$, the sample complexity of multicalibration remains $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ exhibiting a sharp threshold phenomenon.
  More generally, we establish matching upper and lower bounds, up to polylogarithmic factors, for a weighted $L_p$ multicalibration metric for all $1 \le p \le 2$, with optimal exponent $3/p$. We also extend the lower-bound template to a regular class of elicitable properties, and combine it with the online upper bounds of Hu et al. (2025) to obtain matching bounds for calibrating properties including expectiles and bounded-density quantiles.

</details>


### [149] [Regime-Calibrated Demand Priors for Ride-Hailing Fleet Dispatch and Repositioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03883)
*Indar Kumar,Akanksha Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于需求状态标定的网约车调度方法，通过将历史出行数据分割为需求状态、使用多种相似性度量组合匹配当前运营时段、并基于标定的需求先验驱动LP重定位和匈牙利匹配批量调度，在纽约5.2百万次行程上实现31.1%的平均等待时间降低，无需训练、可解释、可泛化至芝加哥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车调度需要预测随时间（时段、星期、季节、特殊事件）大幅变化的需求模式，现有方法难以有效捕捉这种动态变化。

Method: (i) 将历史出行数据分割为需求状态（regimes）；(ii) 通过包含Kolmogorov-Smirnov距离、Wasserstein-1距离、特征距离、方差比、事件模式相似性和时间邻近性的集成相似性度量，匹配当前运营时段与最相似的历史时期；(iii) 利用标定的需求先验驱动基于LP的车队重定位策略和基于匈牙利匹配的批量调度。

Result: 在纽约5.2百万次行程8种场景下，平均等待时间降低31.1%（95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]），P95等待时间降低37.6%，Gini系数从0.441改善至0.409。标定贡献16.9%降低，LP重定位额外贡献15.5%。泛化至芝加哥实现23.3%等待降低，车队规模鲁棒（0.5x-2.0x下32-47%改善）。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需训练、确定性强、可解释，在多个城市和车队规模下均表现出显著且鲁棒的等待时间降低效果。

Abstract: Effective ride-hailing dispatch requires anticipating demand patterns that vary substantially across time-of-day, day-of-week, season, and special events. We propose a regime-calibrated approach that (i) segments historical trip data into demand regimes, (ii) matches the current operating period to the most similar historical analogues via a similarity ensemble combining Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance, Wasserstein-1 distance, feature distance, variance ratio, event pattern similarity, and temporal proximity, and (iii) uses the resulting calibrated demand prior to drive both an LP-based fleet repositioning policy and batch dispatch with Hungarian matching. In ablation, a distributional-only metric subset achieves the strongest mean-wait reduction, while the full ensemble is retained as a robustness-oriented default that preserves calendar and event context.
  Evaluated on 5.2 million NYC TLC trips across 8 diverse scenarios (winter/summer, weekday/weekend/holiday, morning/evening/night) with 5 random seeds each, our method reduces mean rider wait times by 31.1% (bootstrap 95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]; Friedman chi-squared = 80.0, p = 4.25e-18; Cohen's d = 7.5-29.9). P95 wait drops 37.6% and the Gini coefficient of wait times improves from 0.441 to 0.409. The two contributions compose multiplicatively: calibration provides 16.9% reduction relative to the replay baseline; LP repositioning adds a further 15.5%. The approach requires no training, is deterministic and explainable, generalizes to Chicago (23.3% wait reduction using the NYC-built regime library without retraining), and is robust across fleet sizes (32-47% improvement for 0.5x-2.0x fleet scaling). Code is available at https://github.com/IndarKarhana/regime-calibrated-dispatch.

</details>


### [150] [Supervised Dimensionality Reduction Revisited: Why LDA on Frozen CNN Features Deserves a Second Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03928)
*Indar Kumar,Girish Karhana,Sai Krishna Jasti,Ankit Hemant Lade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于需求状态校准的网约车调度方法，通过历史数据分群、多指标相似性匹配（六维指标集成）生成校准需求先验，并驱动LP车队重定位与匈牙利匹配批次派单。在520万行程数据上，平均等待时间降低31.1%，P95等待降低37.6%，基尼系数改善7.3%。方法无需训练、可解释、可泛化至芝加哥场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车调度需要预测随时间、星期、季节及特殊事件变化的需求模式，现有方法难以有效利用历史数据中的结构信息进行需求校准。

Method: (i) 将历史行程数据分割为需求状态(regimes)；(ii) 使用六指标相似性集成（Kolmogorov-Smirnov、Wasserstein-1、特征距离、方差比、事件模式、时间接近度）将当前运营时段匹配到最相似的历史模拟；(iii) 利用校准后的需求先验驱动LP车队重定位策略与匈牙利匹配批量派单。

Result: 在520万纽约TLC行程、8种场景、各5种随机种子下：平均等待时间降低31.1%（95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]%，Friedman检验p=4.25e-18，Cohen's d=7.5-29.9）；P95等待降低37.6%；基尼系数从0.441降至0.409（改善7.3%）。校准贡献16.9%改善，LP重定位贡献15.5%，两者乘性叠加。无需训练，可解释，泛化至芝加哥（23.3%等待降低），对车队规模鲁棒（0.5-2倍规模下32-47%改善）。

Conclusion: 基于历史需求状态校准的方法能有效提升网约车调度效率，具有无需训练、确定性可解释、可跨城市泛化等优势，在多种场景下均表现出显著且稳健的等待时间改善效果。

Abstract: Effective ride-hailing dispatch requires anticipating demand patterns that vary substantially across time-of-day, day-of-week, season, and special events. We propose a regime-calibrated approach that (i) segments historical trip data into demand regimes, (ii) matches the current operating period to the most similar historical analogues via a six-metric similarity ensemble (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, Wasserstein-1, feature distance, variance ratio, event pattern, temporal proximity), and (iii) uses the resulting calibrated demand prior to drive both an LP-based fleet repositioning policy and batch dispatch with Hungarian matching. In ablation, a distributional-only subset is strongest on mean wait, while the full ensemble is retained as a robustness-oriented default.
  Evaluated on 5.2 million NYC TLC trips across 8 diverse scenarios (winter/summer, weekday/weekend/holiday, morning/evening/night) with 5 random seeds each, our method reduces mean rider wait times by 31.1% (bootstrap 95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]%; Friedman chi-sq = 80.0, p = 4.25e-18; Cohen's d = 7.5-29.9 across scenarios). The improvement extends to the tail: P95 wait drops 37.6% and the Gini coefficient of wait times improves from 0.441 to 0.409 (7.3% relative). The two contributions compose multiplicatively and are independently validated: calibration provides 16.9% reduction; LP repositioning adds a further 15.5%. The approach requires no training, is deterministic and explainable, generalizes to Chicago (23.3% wait reduction via NYC-built regime library), and is robust across fleet sizes (32-47% improvement for 0.5-2x fleet scaling). We provide comprehensive ablation studies, formal statistical tests, and routing-fidelity validation with OSRM.

</details>


### [151] [Differentially Private Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20985)
*Qichuan Yin,Manzil Zaheer,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种后处理技术（随机选择和线性组合），利用一组已有不同隐私/效用权衡的模型，在不额外训练的情况下生成满足任意差分隐私要求的模型，并从Rényi DP和隐私损失分布角度进行了理论分析和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习应用中，推理或部署时的隐私需求会因政策、法规或用户体验变化而不断变化，现有方法无法灵活适应任意目标隐私参数，需要一种无需重新训练的解决方案。

Method: 提出两种后处理技术：1) 随机选择（random selection）：从已有模型池中随机选择一个模型；2) 线性组合（linear combination）：对多个已有模型进行线性加权组合。从Rényi DP和隐私损失分布角度进行隐私核算，并在私有均值估计场景中完整刻画隐私/效用结果。

Result: 理论上证明了在私有均值估计中线性组合优于随机选择；在合成和真实数据集上的多个模型上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的后处理方法能够灵活适应变化的隐私需求，无需额外训练，其中线性组合方法在理论上和实践中都表现更优。

Abstract: In machine learning applications, privacy requirements during inference or deployment time could change constantly due to varying policies, regulations, or user experience. In this work, we aim to generate a magnitude of models to satisfy any target differential privacy (DP) requirement without additional training steps, given a set of existing models trained on the same dataset with different privacy/utility tradeoffs. We propose two post processing techniques, namely random selection and linear combination, to output a final private model for any target privacy parameter. We provide privacy accounting of these approaches from the lens of R'enyi DP and privacy loss distributions for general problems. In a case study on private mean estimation, we fully characterize the privacy/utility results and theoretically establish the superiority of linear combination over random selection. Empirically, we validate our approach and analyses on several models and both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [152] [Even More Guarantees for Variational Inference in the Presence of Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21407)
*Lena Zellinger,Antonio Vergari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了变分推断中当变分族不包含目标分布时，能否恢复目标分布特征的问题。作者扩展了先前关于位置-尺度族在目标对称性下鲁棒VI的结果，推导了使用前向KL散度和α-散度时能够精确恢复均值的充分条件，并分析了优化失败的原因，为变分族选择和α值设定提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，变分推断通常使用简单的参数化变分族，这些族很可能不包含真实目标分布（即模型设定错误）。因此，一个重要的问题是：在这种错误设定下，我们能否以及如何恢复目标分布的关键特征（如均值）。

Method: 作者在位置-尺度变分族框架下，利用目标分布的对称性，推导了使用前向KL散度和α-散度时精确恢复目标均值的充分条件。同时，通过分析优化失败的情形来揭示这些条件的重要性。

Result: 给出了在模型设定错误下使用前向KL散度和α-散度能够精确恢复目标均值的充分条件；展示了当这些条件不满足时优化可能失败的原因和具体表现。

Conclusion: 为变分推断中变分族的选择和α-散度中α值的设定提供了初步指导原则，帮助实践者在模型设定错误的情况下仍能可靠地恢复目标分布的关键特征。

Abstract: When approximating an intractable density via variational inference (VI) the variational family is typically chosen as a simple parametric family that very likely does not contain the target. This raises the question: Under which conditions can we recover characteristics of the target despite misspecification? In this work, we extend previous results on robust VI with location-scale families under target symmetries. We derive sufficient conditions guaranteeing exact recovery of the mean when using the forward Kullback-Leibler divergence and $α$-divergences. We further show how and why optimization can fail to recover the target mean in the absence of our sufficient conditions, providing initial guidelines on the choice of the variational family and $α$-value.

</details>


### [153] [Frequency-Forcing: From Scaling-as-Time to Soft Frequency Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20902)
*Weitao Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Frequency-Forcing，通过轻量级可学习小波包变换生成的低频辅助流来引导标准像素流，在不改写核心流坐标的情况下实现了频率排序生成，在ImageNet-256上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，K-Flow通过硬频率约束实现排序但需改写轨迹空间，Latent Forcing提供软排序机制但依赖重预训练编码器（如DINO）。本文希望结合两者优势，实现无需外部依赖的软频率排序。

Method: 提出Frequency-Forcing：保留标准像素流不变，引入一个从数据自身通过轻量级可学习小波包变换导出的低频辅助流（self-forcing信号），该辅助流在时间上提前成熟，通过异步时间调度引导像素流生成顺序。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，Frequency-Forcing一致性地优于强像素空间和潜空间基线；并且可自然地与语义流组合以获得进一步提升。

Conclusion: 基于forcing的尺度排序是一种通用的、保路径的替代硬频率流的方法，通过自引导信号避免了外部依赖，同时学习比固定基更适应数据统计的基。

Abstract: While standard flow-matching models transport noise to data uniformly, incorporating an explicit generation order - specifically, establishing coarse, low-frequency structure before fine detail - has proven highly effective for synthesizing natural images. Two recent works offer distinct paradigms for this. K-Flow imposes a hard frequency constraint by reinterpreting a frequency scaling variable as flow time, running the trajectory inside a transformed amplitude space. Latent Forcing provides a soft ordering mechanism by coupling the pixel flow with an auxiliary semantic latent flow via asynchronous time schedules, leaving the pixel interpolation path itself untouched. Viewed from the angle of improving pixel generation, we observe that forcing - guiding generation with an earlier-maturing auxiliary stream - offers a highly compatible route to scale-ordered generation without rewriting the core flow coordinate. Building on this, we propose Frequency-Forcing, which realizes K-Flow's frequency ordering through Latent Forcing's soft mechanism: a standard pixel flow is guided by an auxiliary low-frequency stream that matures earlier in time. Unlike Latent Forcing, whose scratchpad relies on a heavy pretrained encoder (e.g., DINO), our frequency scratchpad is derived from the data itself via a lightweight learnable wavelet packet transform. We term this a self-forcing signal, which avoids external dependencies while learning a basis better adapted to data statistics than the fixed bases used in hard frequency flows. On ImageNet-256, Frequency-Forcing consistently improves FID over strong pixel- and latent-space baselines, and naturally composes with a semantic stream to yield further gains. This illustrates that forcing-based scale ordering is a versatile, path-preserving alternative to hard frequency flows.

</details>


### [154] [Quotient-Space Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21809)
*Yixian Xu,Yusong Wang,Shengjie Luo,Kaiyuan Gao,Tianyu He,Di He,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个适用于一般商空间的扩散建模框架，并将其应用于具有SE(3)对称性的分子结构生成中。该框架通过减少对群作用对应分量的学习需求，简化了学习难度，同时保证采样器能恢复目标分布。实验证明，该商空间扩散模型在小分子和蛋白质结构生成上优于传统的群等变扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多科学任务中，系统存在对称性（如SE(3)对称性），目标分布本质上定义在商空间上。传统群等变扩散模型需要学习群作用对应的分量，增加了学习难度，且启发式对齐策略缺乏合适的采样器。因此需要建立一种正式的商空间扩散建模框架来简化学习并保证采样正确性。

Method: 建立了一个适用于一般商空间的扩散模型形式化框架，利用商空间的结构特性，将群作用对应的分量从学习目标中剥离，从而简化学习任务。该框架保证了采样器能够正确恢复目标分布，而不需要启发式的对齐策略。

Result: 在小分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上的实验验证表明，提出的商空间扩散模型优于传统群等变扩散模型和启发式对齐方法。

Conclusion: 商空间扩散模型为具有对称性的科学生成任务提供了一种全新的、原则性的框架，能够更有效地处理SE(3)等对称性约束，在分子结构生成任务中表现更优。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have reformed generative AI, and have enabled new capabilities in the science domain, for example, generating 3D structures of molecules. Due to the intrinsic problem structure of certain tasks, there is often a symmetry in the system, which identifies objects that can be converted by a group action as equivalent, hence the target distribution is essentially defined on the quotient space with respect to the group. In this work, we establish a formal framework for diffusion modeling on a general quotient space, and apply it to molecular structure generation which follows the special Euclidean group $\text{SE}(3)$ symmetry. The framework reduces the necessity of learning the component corresponding to the group action, hence simplifies learning difficulty over conventional group-equivariant diffusion models, and the sampler guarantees recovering the target distribution, while heuristic alignment strategies lack proper samplers. The arguments are empirically validated on structure generation for small molecules and proteins, indicating that the principled quotient-space diffusion model provides a new framework that outperforms previous symmetry treatments.

</details>


### [155] [Do Masked Autoencoders Improve Downhole Prediction? An Empirical Study on Real Well Drilling Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20909)
*Aleksander Berezowski,Hassan Hassanzadeh,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练在下钻钻井指标预测中的效果，基于 Utah FORGE 两口地热井约350万时间步数据，在72种MAE配置中最佳方案相比监督GRU基线降低了19.8%的测试MAE，但落后监督LSTM基线6.4%。研究发现潜空间宽度是主导架构因素，而掩码比例影响可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下钻钻井遥测存在根本性的标注不对称：地面传感器数据连续生成（1Hz），而标注的下钻测量数据成本高昂、间歇且稀缺。现有机器学习方法普遍采用从零开始的完全监督训练，不适合这种数据模式。

Method: 使用两个公开的Utah FORGE地热井数据集（约350万时间步的多变量钻井遥测数据），进行72种MAE配置的全因子设计空间搜索，并与监督LSTM和GRU基线在总泥浆体积预测任务上进行比较。

Result: 最佳MAE配置相比监督GRU基线测试MAE降低19.8%，但落后监督LSTM基线6.4%。潜空间宽度是主导架构选择（Pearson r=-0.59），掩码比例影响可忽略（归因于1Hz钻井数据的高时间冗余性）。

Conclusion: MAE预训练可作为钻井分析的有效范式，研究识别了其最有利的应用条件。

Abstract: Downhole drilling telemetry presents a fundamental labeling asymmetry: surface sensor data are generated continuously at 1~Hz, while labeled downhole measurements are costly, intermittent, and scarce. Current machine learning approaches for downhole metric prediction universally adopt fully supervised training from scratch, which is poorly suited to this data regime. We present the first empirical evaluation of masked autoencoder (MAE) pretraining for downhole drilling metric prediction. Using two publicly available Utah FORGE geothermal wells comprising approximately 3.5 million timesteps of multivariate drilling telemetry, we conduct a systematic full-factorial design space search across 72 MAE configurations and compare them against supervised LSTM and GRU baselines on the task of predicting Total Mud Volume. Results show that the best MAE configuration reduces test mean absolute error by 19.8\% relative to the supervised GRU baseline, while trailing the supervised LSTM baseline by 6.4\%. Analysis of design dimensions reveals that latent space width is the dominant architectural choice (Pearson $r = -0.59$ with test MAE), while masking ratio has negligible effect, an unexpected finding attributed to high temporal redundancy in 1~Hz drilling data. These results establish MAE pretraining as a viable paradigm for drilling analytics and identify the conditions under which it is most beneficial.

</details>


### [156] [FairyFuse: Multiplication-Free LLM Inference on CPUs via Fused Ternary Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20913)
*Fei Zuo,Xiaoyan Xi,Quanyi Zeng,Feiyu Wang,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairyFuse是一个面向CPU的推理系统，通过将三元权重（{-1,0,+1}）与AVX-512掩码加减法融合，实现无乘法执行，在Intel Xeon 8558P上达到32.4 tokens/s，比llama.cpp Q4_K_M快1.24倍，质量接近无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅CPU平台上，内存带宽是自回归生成的主要瓶颈。现有4-bit量化系统仍需反量化并进行浮点乘法，限制了性能提升。三元权重可替代乘法为条件加减法，但现有运行时并未利用这一结构优势。

Method: 将每个广泛线性层的8个实值子GEMV融合为单个AVX-512循环，使用掩码加法和减法，零浮点乘法。通过16倍权重压缩将内存受限的GEMV推向计算受限区域。

Result: 单Intel Xeon 8558P上达32.4 tokens/s；相比llama.cpp Q4_K_M加速1.24x；WikiText-2困惑度5.52（FP16为5.47），下游准确率66.0%。Roofline分析显示29.6x内核加速，GPU上收益有限。

Conclusion: FairyFuse通过三元权重的结构化利用和AVX-512指令集融合，在CPU上实现了高效的无乘法推理，为带宽受限的部署场景提供了有竞争力的方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed on CPU-only platforms where memory bandwidth is the primary bottleneck for autoregressive generation. Weight quantization to four bits or below reduces memory pressure, yet existing systems still dequantize weights and perform floating-point multiplications, limiting the achievable gains. Ternary weights in {-1, 0, +1} provide a more efficient alternative, replacing multiplications with conditional additions, subtractions, or no-ops. While Fairy2i shows that ternary LLMs can match FP16 quality, its runtime does not exploit this structure. We present FairyFuse, an inference system that enables multiplication-free execution on commodity CPUs by fusing the eight real-valued sub-GEMVs of each widely-linear layer into a single AVX-512 loop using masked additions and subtractions, with zero floating-point multiplications. Roofline analysis shows that 16x weight compression shifts memory-bound GEMV toward the compute regime on bandwidth-limited CPUs, yielding a 29.6x kernel speedup while offering little benefit on GPUs. End-to-end, FairyFuse achieves 32.4 tokens per second on a single Intel Xeon 8558P, outperforming llama.cpp Q4_K_M by 1.24x with near-lossless quality (WikiText-2 perplexity 5.52 vs. 5.47 FP16; downstream accuracy 66.0%).

</details>


### [157] [Forget, Then Recall: Learnable Compression and Selective Unfolding via Gist Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20920)
*Yuzhen Mao,Michael Y. Li,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GSA（Gist Sparse Attention）方法，通过可学习的gist压缩令牌作为路由信号实现稀疏注意力，在长上下文场景中以粗到细的方式兼顾全局表示与细粒度证据访问，在LongBench和RAG基准上优于多种压缩和稀疏注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型处理长上下文时，全注意力的二次计算成本过高，现有缓解方法（KV缓存选择或压缩技术）仍有局限性，需要一种无需修改架构、端到端可学习的桥接方案。

Method: 提出交错gist压缩令牌作为稀疏注意力的路由信号，通过GSA实现选择性展开：先压缩上下文为gist令牌，选择最相关的gist，再还原对应原始chunk进行细粒度注意力。该方法端到端训练，无需外部检索模块，并扩展为层次化递归gist-of-gist结构实现多分辨率上下文访问。

Result: 在LongBench和RAG基准上，在8倍到32倍压缩比下，GSA一致优于其他压缩基线和推理时稀疏注意力方法。

Conclusion: 通过可学习的gist令牌桥接压缩与稀疏注意力，实现了高效的长上下文建模，且层次化扩展进一步降低了每步解码复杂度（对数级别）。

Abstract: Scaling large language models to long contexts is challenging due to the quadratic computational cost of full attention. Mitigation approaches include KV-cache selection or compression techniques. We instead provide an effective and end-to-end learnable bridge between the two without requiring architecture modification. In particular, our key insight is that interleaved gist compression tokens -- which provide a learnable summary of sets of raw tokens -- can serve as routing signals for sparse attention. Building on this, we introduce selective unfolding via GSA, which first compresses the context into gist tokens, then selects the most relevant gists, and subsequently restores the corresponding raw chunks for detailed attention. This yields a simple coarse-to-fine mechanism that combines compact global representations with targeted access to fine-grained evidence. We further incorporate this process directly into training in an end-to-end fashion, avoiding the need for external retrieval modules. In addition, we extend the framework hierarchically via recursive gist-of-gist construction, enabling multi-resolution context access with logarithmic per-step decoding complexity. Empirical results on LongBench and RAG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms other compression baselines as well as inference-time sparse attention methods across compression ratios from $8\times$ to $32\times$. The code is available at: https://github.com/yuzhenmao/gist-sparse-attention/

</details>


### [158] [Validating a Deep Learning Algorithm to Identify Patients with Glaucoma using Systemic Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20921)
*John Xiang,Rohith Ravindranath,Sophia Y. Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用美国All of Us国家数据训练的青光眼风险评估（GRA）模型，仅通过全身性电子健康记录（EHR）数据即可在独立机构识别高风险患者。在20,636名Stanford患者（15%患青光眼）中，最优模型AUROC达0.883，PPV达0.657，预测最高十分位组青光眼诊断率65.7%、治疗率57.0%，校准良好。性能随可训练层数（最多15层）和数据量增加而提升。该EHR-only模型无需专门影像即可实现可扩展、可及的青光眼预筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统青光眼筛查依赖专门影像设备（如OCT、视野检查），成本高、可及性差，不适合大规模人群筛查。作者希望利用广泛可得的系统性EHR数据（包括人口学、诊断、用药、实验室和体检信息），开发无需专门眼科检查即可识别青光眼高风险患者的模型，从而提升筛查的可扩展性和可及性。

Method: 横断面研究。纳入2013年11月至2024年1月间20,636名Stanford患者（15%患青光眼）。使用All of Us国家数据预训练的GRA模型，在Stanford队列上进行微调，并在留出测试集上评估。模型输入包括人口学特征、全身性诊断、用药、实验室检查和体检测量结果。评估指标包括AUROC、PPV、校准性能，并分析不同可训练层数和数据量对性能的影响。

Result: 最佳模型AUROC为0.883，PPV为0.657。校准结果与临床风险一致：预测最高十分位组青光眼诊断率65.7%，治疗率57.0%。模型性能随可训练层数（最多15层）和数据量增加而提升。

Conclusion: 仅基于EHR数据的GRA模型能够有效识别青光眼高风险患者，无需专门影像检查，可能实现可扩展、可及的大规模预筛查。

Abstract: We evaluated whether a glaucoma risk assessment (GRA) model trained on All of Us national data can identify patients at high probability of glaucoma using only systemic electronic health records (EHR) at an independent institution. In this cross-sectional study, 20,636 Stanford patients seen from November 2013 to January 2024 were included (15% with glaucoma). A pretrained GRA model was fine-tuned on the Stanford cohort and tested on a held-out set using demographics, systemic diagnoses, medications, laboratory results, and physical examination measurements as inputs. The best model achieved AUROC 0.883 and PPV 0.657. Calibration was consistent with clinical risk: the highest prediction decile showed the greatest glaucoma diagnosis rate (65.7%) and treatment rate (57.0%). Performance improved with more trainable layers up to 15 and with additional data. An EHR-only GRA model may enable scalable and accessible pre-screening without specialized imaging.

</details>


### [159] [Clinically Interpretable Sepsis Early Warning via LLM-Guided Simulation of Temporal Physiological Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20924)
*Weizhi Nie,Zhen Qu,Weijie Wang,Chunpei Li,Ke Lu,Bingyang Zhou,Hongzhi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型引导的时间模拟框架，通过显式建模脓毒症发病前的生理轨迹实现可解释的早期预警，在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据库上达到0.861-0.903的AUC，优于传统深度学习和基于规则的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动的脓毒症早期预警模型虽然准确但缺乏可解释性，限制了临床医生的信任度和临床应用。亟需一种既能提供准确预测又能给出符合临床判断的可解释预测机制的方法。

Method: 提出LLM引导的时间模拟框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 时空特征提取模块，捕获多变量生命体征的动态依赖关系；(2) 医疗提示作为前缀模块，将临床推理线索嵌入LLM；(3) 基于智能体的后处理组件，将预测约束在生理学合理范围内。该方法先模拟关键生理指标的演变，再分类脓毒症发作。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据库上，该方法在发病前24-4小时的预测任务中实现了0.861-0.903的AUC，优于传统深度学习方法和基于规则的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了可解释的生理轨迹和风险趋势，能够辅助临床医生在重症监护环境中进行早期干预和个性化决策。

Abstract: Timely and interpretable early warning of sepsis remains a major clinical challenge due to the complex temporal dynamics of physiological deterioration. Traditional data-driven models often provide accurate yet opaque predictions, limiting physicians' confidence and clinical applicability. To address this limitation, we propose a Large Language Model (LLM)-guided temporal simulation framework that explicitly models physiological trajectories prior to disease onset for clinically interpretable prediction. The framework consists of a spatiotemporal feature extraction module that captures dynamic dependencies among multivariate vital signs, a Medical Prompt-as-Prefix module that embeds clinical reasoning cues into LLMs, and an agent-based post-processing component that constrains predictions within physiologically plausible ranges. By first simulating the evolution of key physiological indicators and then classifying sepsis onset, our model offers transparent prediction mechanisms that align with clinical judgment. Evaluated on the MIMIC-IV and eICU databases, the proposed method achieves superior AUC scores (0.861-0.903) across 24-4-hour pre-onset prediction tasks, outperforming conventional deep learning and rule-based approaches. More importantly, it provides interpretable trajectories and risk trends that can assist clinicians in early intervention and personalized decision-making in intensive care environments.

</details>


### [160] [Domain-Aware Hierarchical Contrastive Learning for Semi-Supervised Generalization Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20928)
*Junyu Ren,Wensheng Gan,Philip S Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出域感知层次对比学习(DAHCL)框架，通过域感知学习模块校正跨域伪标签偏差，以及层次对比学习模块对不确定样本进行模糊对比监督，从而解决半监督域泛化故障诊断中伪标签偏差和硬阈值丢弃策略导致的样本利用不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督域泛化故障诊断方法存在两个耦合局限：(1) 伪标签生成主要依赖标记源域知识，忽略了域特定的几何差异，导致系统性跨域伪标签偏差；(2) 对未标记样本采用硬接受或丢弃策略，刚性阈值导致跨域样本利用不平衡，硬标签分配对不确定样本引入额外噪声。

Method: 提出DAHCL统一框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 域感知学习(DAL)模块，显式捕捉源域几何特征并校正跨异构源域的伪标签预测，缓解伪标签生成中的跨域偏差；(2) 层次对比学习(HCL)模块，结合动态置信度分层与模糊对比监督，使不确定样本无需依赖不可靠硬标签即可参与表示学习。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上进行大量实验，表明DAHCL在半监督域泛化故障诊断任务上表现优越。

Conclusion: DAHCL通过联合改进监督质量和未标记样本利用率，有效解决了半监督域泛化故障诊断中的伪标签偏差和样本利用不平衡问题。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis under unseen operating conditions remains highly challenging when labeled data are scarce. Semi-supervised domain generalization fault diagnosis (SSDGFD) provides a practical solution by jointly exploiting labeled and unlabeled source domains. However, existing methods still suffer from two coupled limitations. First, pseudo-labels for unlabeled domains are typically generated primarily from knowledge learned on the labeled source domain, which neglects domain-specific geometric discrepancies and thus induces systematic cross-domain pseudo-label bias. Second, unlabeled samples are commonly handled with a hard accept-or-discard strategy, where rigid thresholding causes imbalanced sample utilization across domains, while hard-label assignment for uncertain samples can easily introduce additional noise. To address these issues, we propose a unified framework termed domain-aware hierarchical contrastive learning (DAHCL) for SSDGFD. Specifically, DAHCL introduces a domain-aware learning (DAL) module to explicitly capture source-domain geometric characteristics and calibrate pseudo-label predictions across heterogeneous source domains, thereby mitigating cross-domain bias in pseudo-label generation. In addition, DAHCL develops a hierarchical contrastive learning (HCL) module that combines dynamic confidence stratification with fuzzy contrastive supervision, enabling uncertain samples to contribute to representation learning without relying on unreliable hard labels. In this way, DAHCL jointly improves the quality of supervision and the utilization of unlabeled samples. Furthermore, to better reflect practical industrial scenarios, we incorporate engineering noise into the SSDGFD evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that...

</details>


### [161] [Data-Driven Open-Loop Simulation for Digital-Twin Operator Decision Support in Wastewater Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20935)
*Gary Simethy,Daniel Ortiz Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CCSS-RS，一种受控连续时间状态空间模型，用于污水处理厂数字孪生决策支持，在全规模基准上较基线方法降低RMSE 40-46%，并验证了操作价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 污水处理厂需要数字孪生决策支持工具，能够模拟控制计划下的工厂响应、容忍不规则和缺失的传感数据，并在12-36小时规划窗口内保持信息有效，但用全规模工厂数据满足这些需求仍是工程-AI挑战。

Method: CCSS-RS将历史状态推断与未来控制和外部变量展开分离，采用类型化上下文编码、增益加权驱动、半群一致展开，以及Student-t加障碍输出处理重尾和零膨胀的WWTP传感器数据。

Result: 在Avedøre全规模基准上（906,815时间步，43%缺失率，1-20分钟不规则采样），H=1000时RMSE 0.696，CRPS 0.349。相对Neural CDE基线降低RMSE 40-46%，相对简化变体降低31-35%。四个案例研究验证了操作价值。

Conclusion: CCSS-RS可作为工业污水处理离线场景筛选的实用学习模拟器，与机理模型互补。

Abstract: Wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) need digital-twin-style decision support tools that can simulate plant response under prescribed control plans, tolerate irregular and missing sensing, and remain informative over 12-36 h planning horizons. Meeting these requirements with full-scale plant data remains an open engineering-AI challenge. We present CCSS-RS, a controlled continuous-time state-space model that separates historical state inference from future control and exogenous rollout. The model combines typed context encoding, gain-weighted forcing of prescribed and forecast drivers, semigroup-consistent rollouts, and Student-t plus hurdle outputs for heavy-tailed and zero-inflated WWTP sensor data. On the public Avedøre full-scale benchmark, with 906,815 timesteps, 43% missingness, and 1-20 min irregular sampling, CCSS-RS achieves RMSE 0.696 and CRPS 0.349 at H=1000 across 10,000 test windows. This reduces RMSE by 40-46% relative to Neural CDE baselines and by 31-35% relative to simplified internal variants. Four case studies using a frozen checkpoint on test data demonstrate operational value: oxygen-setpoint perturbations shift predicted ammonium by -2.3 to +1.4 over horizons 300-1000; a smoothed setpoint plan ranks first in multi-criterion screening; context-only sensor outages raise monitored-variable RMSE by at most 10%; and ammonium, nitrate, and oxygen remain more accurate than persistence throughout the rollout. These results establish CCSS-RS as a practical learned simulator for offline scenario screening in industrial wastewater treatment, complementary to mechanistic models.

</details>


### [162] [Sink-Token-Aware Pruning for Fine-Grained Video Understanding in Efficient Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20937)
*Kibum Kim,Jiwan Kim,Kyle Min,Yueqi Wang,Jinyoung Moon,Julian McAuley,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有视频大语言模型的训练无关视觉令牌剪枝方法在细粒度理解任务（如幻觉评估）上性能急剧下降的问题，发现原因是“沉没令牌”（sink tokens）在剪枝后扭曲了模型视觉证据。为此提出SToP方法，通过引入沉没分数抑制这类令牌，在剪枝高达90%视觉令牌时仍能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关视觉令牌剪枝方法主要在粗粒度的多项选择问答（MCQA）基准上验证，在需要精确视觉定位的细粒度理解任务（如幻觉评估）上性能严重下降，缺乏对此问题的系统分析及解决方案。

Method: 提出SToP（Sink-Token-aware Pruning），一种即插即用的方法。通过引入沉没分数（sink score）量化每个令牌成为沉没令牌的趋势，并将该分数应用于现有的空间和时间剪枝方法中，抑制沉没令牌的保留。

Result: 将SToP应用于VisionZip、FastVid和Holitom等先进剪枝方法，在覆盖幻觉评估、开放生成、组合推理和MCQA的多样化基准上评估，结果表明SToP显著提升了性能，即使在剪枝高达90%视觉令牌时仍有效。

Conclusion: 沉没令牌是影响细粒度视频理解的关键障碍，SToP通过感知并抑制沉没令牌，有效解决了现有剪枝方法在细粒度任务上的性能崩塌问题，是一种简单有效的通用改进方案。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) incur high inference latency due to a large number of visual tokens provided to LLMs. To address this, training-free visual token pruning has emerged as a solution to reduce computational costs; however, existing methods are primarily validated on Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) benchmarks, where coarse-grained cues often suffice. In this work, we reveal that these methods suffer a sharp performance collapse on fine-grained understanding tasks requiring precise visual grounding, such as hallucination evaluation. To explore this gap, we conduct a systematic analysis and identify sink tokens--semantically uninformative tokens that attract excessive attention--as a key obstacle to fine-grained video understanding. When these sink tokens survive pruning, they distort the model's visual evidence and hinder fine-grained understanding. Motivated by these insights, we propose Sink-Token-aware Pruning (SToP), a simple yet effective plug-and-play method that introduces a sink score to quantify each token's tendency to behave as a sink and applies this score to existing spatial and temporal pruning methods to suppress them, thereby enhancing video understanding. To validate the effectiveness of SToP, we apply it to state-of-the-art pruning methods (VisionZip, FastVid, and Holitom) and evaluate it across diverse benchmarks covering hallucination, open-ended generation, compositional reasoning, and MCQA. Our results demonstrate that SToP significantly boosts performance, even when pruning up to 90% of visual tokens.

</details>


### [163] [SCM: Sleep-Consolidated Memory with Algorithmic Forgetting for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20943)
*Saish Sachin Shinde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCM（睡眠巩固记忆）架构，一种受神经科学启发的LLM记忆系统，包含工作记忆、重要性标记、NREM/REM睡眠阶段巩固、价值导向遗忘和自省模型五个核心组件。在八项测试基准中，该原型在十轮对话中达到完美召回准确率，通过自适应遗忘将记忆噪声降低90.9%，记忆搜索延迟低于1毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM记忆系统存在根本性局限：缺乏持久、结构化且具有生物合理性的记忆机制。现有方法依赖截断上下文窗口、无界增长的向量数据库或缺乏巩固和遗忘机制的分层存储系统。

Method: SCM架构包含五个核心组件：（1）有限容量工作记忆；（2）多维度重要性标记；（3）离线的NREM和REM睡眠阶段巩固过程；（4）基于价值的意向性遗忘；（5）支持内省的计算自我模型。

Result: 在标准化基准测试套件中，SCM实现十轮对话的完美召回准确率；通过自适应遗忘将记忆噪声降低90.9%；即使存储数百个概念，记忆搜索延迟仍低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了能够巩固、优先排序和遗忘的记忆系统架构基础，为推进LLM记忆研究提供了可测试的平台。

Abstract: We present SCM (Sleep-Consolidated Memory), a research preview of a memory architecture for large language models that draws on neuroscientific principles to address a fundamental limitation in current systems: the absence of persistent, structured, and biologically plausible memory. Existing approaches rely on truncating context windows, growing vector databases without bound, or tiered storage systems that lack consolidation and forgetting mechanisms. SCM implements five core components inspired by human memory: a limited-capacity working memory, multi-dimensional importance tagging, offline sleep-stage consolidation with distinct NREM and REM phases, intentional value-based forgetting, and a computational self-model enabling introspection. Across a standardized benchmark suite of eight tests, the prototype achieves perfect recall accuracy over ten-turn conversations while reducing memory noise by 90.9% through adaptive forgetting. Memory search latency remains below one millisecond even with hundreds of stored concepts. This work establishes the architectural foundations for memory systems that consolidate, prioritize, and forget, offering a testable platform for advancing LLM memory research.

</details>


### [164] [MCAP: Deployment-Time Layer Profiling for Memory-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21026)
*Anurita Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCAP（蒙特卡洛激活分析）方法，在加载时评估每层重要性，实现动态精度分配（W4A8 vs W4A16）和内存层级决策（GPU/RAM/SSD），构建NVE系统，在NVIDIA T4上相比llama.cpp Q4_0实现1.5-1.8倍解码吞吐提升，使模型能在之前不可行的内存预算下运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在异构硬件上部署时通常受内存限制而非算力限制，现有方法无法在无需修改权重的情况下根据内存预算动态调整精度和存储层级。

Method: 提出MCAP（Monte Carlo Activation Profiling）负载时每层重要性估计器，通过蒙特卡洛方法对激活值进行采样分析，生成轻量级每层信号，驱动精度调度（W4A8与W4A16）和驻留层级（GPU、RAM、SSD）决策。

Result: NVE系统在NVIDIA T4上相比llama.cpp Q4_0实现1.5-1.8倍更高的解码吞吐量，使模型能在之前不可行的内存预算下运行。

Conclusion: MCAP方法使单一权重集能够适应不同内存预算，通过动态精度和存储层级分配有效缓解了LLM部署中的内存瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Deploying large language models to heterogeneous hardware is often constrained by memory, not compute. We introduce MCAP (Monte Carlo Activation Profiling), a load-time per-layer importance estimator that enables dynamic precision and memory placement decisions on the target device. MCAP produces a lightweight per-layer signal that drives both precision dispatch (W4A8 vs. W4A16) and residency tier (GPU, RAM, SSD), allowing a single set of weights to operate across diverse memory budgets. Our system, NVE, achieves 1.5-1.8x higher decode throughput than llama.cpp Q4_0 on NVIDIA T4 and enables models to run in memory regimes previously infeasible without modifying weights.

</details>


### [165] [A Deep U-Net Framework for Flood Hazard Mapping Using Hydraulic Simulations of the Wupper Catchment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21028)
*Christian Lammers,Fernando Arévalo,Leonie Märker-Neuhaus,Daniel Heinenberg,Christian Förster,Karl-Heinz Spies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的替代模型（U-Net架构），用于高效预测洪水期间的最大水位，在德国Wupper流域的测试中取得了与传统水动力模拟相当的结果，但计算效率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球洪水事件日益频繁和严重，传统水动力模拟计算成本高昂，亟需开发快速可靠的洪水预测工具。

Method: 通过系列实验优化U-Net架构、图块生成和数据预处理，构建深度学习替代模型来近似水动力模型，预测网格上的最大水位。

Result: 在德国北莱茵-威斯特法伦州Wupper流域的测试中，该替代模型获得了与传统水动力模拟可比的结果。

Conclusion: 深度学习替代模型可以作为传统水动力模拟的计算高效替代方案，在保证预测精度的同时大幅提升计算效率。

Abstract: The increasing frequency and severity of global flood events highlights the need for the development of rapid and reliable flood prediction tools. This process traditionally relies on computationally expensive hydraulic simulations. This research presents a prediction tool by developing a deep-learning based surrogate model to accurately and efficiently predict the maximum water level across a grid. This was achieved by conducting a series of experiments to optimize a U-Net architecture, patch generation, and data handling for approximating a hydraulic model. This research demonstrates that a deep learning surrogate model can serve as a computationally efficient alternative to traditional hydraulic simulations. The framework was tested using hydraulic simulations of the Wupper catchment in the North-Rhein Westphalia region (Germany), obtaining comparable results.

</details>


### [166] [Interpretable Quantile Regression by Optimal Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21042)
*Valentin Lemaire,Gaël Aglin,Siegfried Nijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种学习最优分位数回归树集合的新方法，能在不牺牲算法效率的前提下提供可解释的、完整的条件分布预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习领域对既准确又可解释且鲁棒的模型需求日益增长，以使终端用户能够理解和信任AI系统。

Method: 提出一种学习最优分位数回归树集合的新方法，该方法同时优化多棵分位数回归树，保持与单棵树相当的计算效率。

Result: 该方法能提供关于目标变量完整条件分布的预测，无需对分布做先验假设；预测具有可解释性；相比学习单棵树，算法效率不妥协。

Conclusion: 该方法在准确性、可解释性和效率之间取得了良好平衡，为构建可信AI系统提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The field of machine learning is subject to an increasing interest in models that are not only accurate but also interpretable and robust, thus allowing their end users to understand and trust AI systems. This paper presents a novel method for learning a set of optimal quantile regression trees. The advantages of this method are that (1) it provides predictions about the complete conditional distribution of a target variable without prior assumptions on this distribution; (2) it provides predictions that are interpretable; (3) it learns a set of optimal quantile regression trees without compromising algorithmic efficiency compared to learning a single tree.

</details>


### [167] [JEPAMatch: Geometric Representation Shaping for Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21046)
*Ali Aghababaei-Harandi,Aude Sportisse,Massih-Reza Amini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个半监督学习新方法，通过引入基于LeJEPA的潜空间正则化项来替代传统的逻辑输出阈值，从而改善FixMatch类方法中存在的类别偏差和早期伪标签噪声问题，在多个数据集上取得了更优性能并加速了收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FixMatch类半监督学习方法存在两个关键瓶颈：(1) 多数类主导学习过程，被错误伪标签放大，导致模型偏差；(2) 早期噪声伪标签阻碍模型形成清晰决策边界，需要长时间训练才能学到有信息量的表示。

Method: 提出一种新的训练目标，将FlexMatch（FixMatch的自适应扩展）中的经典半监督损失与源自LeJEPA的潜空间正则化项相结合。LeJEPA是一个理论上有据可依的框架，认为有意义的表示应在潜空间中呈现各向同性高斯结构。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、STL-10和Tiny-ImageNet上的大量实验表明，所提方法一致优于现有基线。此外，该方法显著加速了收敛，大幅降低了相比标准FixMatch管线的总体计算成本。

Conclusion: 从传统的逻辑输出阈值范式转向显式地塑造几何表示，在半监督学习中是一种有效的范式转变，既能鼓励结构良好的表示，又能保留伪标签策略的优势。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for leveraging large amounts of unlabeled data to improve the performance of machine learning models when labeled data are scarce. Among existing approaches, methods derived from FixMatch have achieved state-of-the-art results in image classification by combining weak and strong data augmentations with confidence-based pseudo-labeling. Despite their strong empirical performance, these methods typically struggle with two critical bottlenecks: majority classes tend to dominate the learning process, which is amplified by incorrect pseudo-labels, leading to biased models. Furthermore, noisy early pseudo-labels prevent the model from forming clear decision boundaries, requiring prolonged training to learn informative representation. In this paper, we introduce a paradigm shift from conventional logical output threshold base, toward an explicit shaping of geometric representations. Our approach is inspired by the recently proposed Latent-Euclidean Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (LeJEPA), a theoretically grounded framework asserting that meaningful representations should exhibit an isotropic Gaussian structure in latent space. Building on this principle, we propose a new training objective that combines the classical semi-supervised loss used in FlexMatch, an adaptive extension of FixMatch, with a latent-space regularization term derived from LeJEPA.
  Our proposed approach, encourages well-structured representations while preserving the advantages of pseudo-labeling strategies. Through extensive experiments on CIFAR-100, STL-10 and Tiny-ImageNet, we demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms existing baselines. In addition, our method significantly accelerates the convergence, drastically reducing the overall computational cost compared to standard FixMatch-based pipelines.

</details>


### [168] [Spectral Embeddings Leak Graph Topology: Theory, Benchmark, and Adaptive Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21094)
*Thinh Nguyen-Cong,Truong-Son Hy,Thang N. Dinh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoGraB（局部图基准测试）和AFR（自适应保真度驱动重构），分别用于图数据碎片化场景的基准测试和高质量图重构，在9个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN基准假设图数据集中可用，但实际场景（联邦学习、分布式系统、隐私敏感应用）中图数据是局部的、碎片化的、带噪声且隐私泄露的。缺乏统一的框架来评估这种碎片化设置下的模型性能。

Method: 提出LoGraB框架，通过三种分解策略和四个控制参数（邻域半径d、频谱质量k、噪声水平σ、覆盖比例p）将标准数据集分解为碎片化基准。提出AFR方法，利用保真度度量（间隙-截断稳定性比+结构熵）评估碎片质量，通过RANSAC-Procrustes对齐、自适应拼接和集束调整重构图岛。给出热核边恢复、Davis-Kahan扰动稳定性和对齐误差有界性的理论保证，以及谱泄露命题。

Result: 在9个基准上实验表明：LoGraB能揭示模型在碎片化下的强弱；AFR在7/9数据集上达到最佳F1；在$(ε,δ)$-高斯差分隐私下，AFR在ε=2时保留75%的无防御F1。

Conclusion: LoGraB为图碎片化场景提供了统一的基准框架，AFR方法在重构保真度、鲁棒性和隐私保护方面均表现出色，为分布式图学习提供了理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) excel on relational data, but standard benchmarks unrealistically assume the graph is centrally available. In practice, settings such as Federated Graph Learning, distributed systems, and privacy-sensitive applications involve graph data that are localized, fragmented, noisy, and privacy-leaking. We present a unified framework for this setting. We introduce LoGraB (Local Graph Benchmark), which decomposes standard datasets into fragmented benchmarks using three strategies and four controls: neighborhood radius $d$, spectral quality $k$, noise level $σ$, and coverage ratio $p$. LoGraB supports graph reconstruction, localized node classification, and inter-fragment link prediction, with Island Cohesion. We propose AFR (Adaptive Fidelity-driven Reconstruction), a method for noisy spectral fragments. AFR scores patch quality via a fidelity measure combining a gap-to-truncation stability ratio and structural entropy, then assembles fragments using RANSAC-Procrustes alignment, adaptive stitching, and Bundle Adjustment. Rather than forcing a single global graph, AFR recovers large faithful islands. We prove heat-kernel edge recovery under a separation condition, Davis--Kahan perturbation stability, and bounded alignment error. We establish a Spectral Leakage Proposition: under a spectral-gap assumption, polynomial-time Bayesian recovery is feasible once enough eigenvectors are shared, complementing AFR's deterministic guarantees. Experiments on nine benchmarks show that LoGraB reveals model strengths and weaknesses under fragmentation, AFR achieves the best F1 on 7/9 datasets, and under per-embedding $(ε,δ)$-Gaussian differential privacy, AFR retains 75% of its undefended F1 at $ε=2$. Our anonymous code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/JMLR_submission

</details>


### [169] [Preconditioned DeltaNet: Curvature-aware Sequence Modeling for Linear Recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21100)
*Neehal Tumma,Noel Loo,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对长上下文场景下softmax注意力的计算限制，提出将预条件技术引入delta规则循环（如DeltaNet、GDN、KDA），从在线最小二乘理论出发推导出精确预条件下的线性注意力与delta规则的等价关系，并通过对角近似实现高效的块并行算法。实验表明，预条件化后的delta规则循环在合成召回基准和340M/1B规模的语言建模中均取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文下softmax注意力存在计算限制，现有次二次循环算子（如Mamba-2、DeltaNet、GDN、KDA）虽能缓解该问题，但现有delta规则循环仅是一阶近似，忽略了最小二乘损失函数在优化过程中的曲率信息，导致优化不足。

Method: 从在线最小二乘理论出发，推导精确预条件下线性注意力与delta规则的等价关系；提出对角近似预条件方案，将预条件技术引入DeltaNet、GDN和KDA中；设计高效的块并行计算算法。

Result: 在合成召回基准和340M/1B规模的语言建模任务上，预条件后的delta规则循环相比原模型取得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 引入预条件能够有效改进delta规则循环的优化质量，为次二次注意力机制提供了新的改进方向。

Abstract: To address the increasing long-context compute limitations of softmax attention, several subquadratic recurrent operators have been developed. This work includes models such as Mamba-2, DeltaNet, Gated DeltaNet (GDN), and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA). As the space of recurrences grows, a parallel line of work has arisen to taxonomize them. One compelling view is the test-time regression (TTR) framework, which interprets recurrences as performing online least squares updates that learn a linear map from the keys to values. Existing delta-rule recurrences can be seen as first-order approximations to this objective, but notably ignore the curvature of the least-squares loss during optimization. In this work, we address this by introducing preconditioning to these recurrences. Starting from the theory of online least squares, we derive equivalences between linear attention and the delta rule in the exactly preconditioned case. Next, we realize this theory in practice by proposing a diagonal approximation: this enables us to introduce preconditioned variants of DeltaNet, GDN, and KDA alongside efficient chunkwise parallel algorithms for computing them. Empirically, we find that our preconditioned delta-rule recurrences yield consistent performance improvements across synthetic recall benchmarks and language modeling at the 340M and 1B scale.

</details>


### [170] [How Much Is One Recurrence Worth? Iso-Depth Scaling Laws for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21106)
*Kristian Schwethelm,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过116次预训练实验，发现深度循环语言模型中每增加一次循环（recurrence）相当于增加约0.46次方的参数等效量（φ=0.46），即循环4次的4.1亿参数模型性能相当于5.8亿参数的非循环模型，但训练成本却相当于10亿参数的非循环模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化循环语言模型中"增加一次循环"与"增加独有参数"之间的等价关系，为循环深度架构的设计提供可预测的损失成本度量。

Method: 对116次预训练实验（循环次数r∈{1,2,4,8}，训练计算量跨度约50倍）进行等深度扫描，拟合联合缩放定律L = E + A·(N_once + r^φ·N_rec)^{-α} + B·D^{-β}，得到循环等价指数φ=0.46（R²=0.997）。

Result: φ=0.46介于0和1之间，表明每次循环的性能增益低于等量独有参数的效果；在五轴下游评估中，参数知识任务上差距明显，简单开卷任务上差距消失，推理任务在现有计算预算下无法分辨。

Conclusion: 循环语言模型的设计选择r可转化为可预测的验证损失成本；未来训练方案和架构可通过其相对于0.46的提升程度进行比较。

Abstract: We measure how much one extra recurrence is worth to a looped (depth-recurrent) language model, in equivalent unique parameters. From an iso-depth sweep of 116 pretraining runs across recurrence counts $r \in \{1, 2, 4, 8\}$ spanning ${\sim}50\times$ in training compute, we fit a joint scaling law $L = E + A\,(N_\text{once} + r^{\varphi} N_\text{rec})^{-α} + B\,D^{-β}$ and recover a new recurrence-equivalence exponent $\varphi = 0.46$ at $R^2 = 0.997$. Intuitively, $\varphi$ tells us whether looping a block $r$ times is equivalent in validation loss to $r$ unique blocks of a non-looped model (full equivalence, $\varphi{=}1$) or to a single block run repeatedly with no capacity gain ($\varphi{=}0$). Our $\varphi = 0.46$ sits in between, so each additional recurrence predictably increases validation loss at matched training compute. For example, at $r{=}4$ a 410M looped model performs on par with a 580M non-looped model, but pays the training cost of a 1B non-looped one. On a five-axis downstream evaluation, the gap persists on parametric-knowledge tasks and closes on simple open-book tasks, while reasoning tasks are not resolvable at our compute budgets. For any looped LM, our $\varphi$ converts the design choice of $r$ into a predictable validation-loss cost, and future training recipes and architectures can be compared by how much they raise $\varphi$ above $0.46$.

</details>


### [171] [Toward Efficient Membership Inference Attacks against Federated Large Language Models: A Projection Residual Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21197)
*Guilin Deng,Silong Chen,Yuchuan Luo,Yi Liu,Songlei Wang,Zhiping Cai,Lin Liu,Xiaohua Jia,Shaojing Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProjRes，首个针对联邦大语言模型（FedLLMs）的基于投影残差的被动成员推断攻击方法，无需影子模型或辅助分类器，通过分析梯度子空间上的嵌入向量投影残差实现近100%的攻击准确率，揭示了FedLLMs中被忽视的隐私漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦大语言模型虽然通过数据本地化保护隐私，但共享梯度仍可能通过成员推断攻击泄露敏感信息。现有MIAs因FedLLMs的大参数规模、快速收敛以及稀疏非正交梯度等特性而失效，亟需设计针对性的攻击方法以重新审视其安全假设。

Method: ProjRes利用隐藏层嵌入向量作为样本表征，计算其在梯度子空间上的投影残差，通过挖掘梯度与输入之间的内在关联实现被动成员推断。该方法无需影子模型、辅助分类器或历史更新记录。

Result: 在四个基准数据集和四个大语言模型上，ProjRes实现了近100%的准确率，较现有方法提升高达75.75%，即使在强差分隐私防御下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: FedLLMs存在此前被忽视的隐私脆弱性，现有安全假设需要重新审视；ProjRes高效鲁棒地揭示了这一漏洞。

Abstract: Federated Large Language Models (FedLLMs) enable multiple parties to collaboratively fine-tune LLMs without sharing raw data, addressing challenges of limited resources and privacy concerns. Despite data localization, shared gradients can still expose sensitive information through membership inference attacks (MIAs). However, FedLLMs' unique properties, i.e. massive parameter scales, rapid convergence, and sparse, non-orthogonal gradients, render existing MIAs ineffective. To address this gap, we propose ProjRes, the first projection residuals-based passive MIA tailored for FedLLMs. ProjRes leverages hidden embedding vectors as sample representations and analyzes their projection residuals on the gradient subspace to uncover the intrinsic link between gradients and inputs. It requires no shadow models, auxiliary classifiers, or historical updates, ensuring efficiency and robustness. Experiments on four benchmarks and four LLMs show that ProjRes achieves near 100% accuracy, outperforming prior methods by up to 75.75%, and remains effective even under strong differential privacy defenses. Our findings reveal a previously overlooked privacy vulnerability in FedLLMs and call for a re-examination of their security assumptions. Our code and data are available at $\href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Passive-MIA-5268}{link}$.

</details>


### [172] [ARFBench: Benchmarking Time Series Question Answering Ability for Software Incident Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21199)
*Stephan Xie,Ben Cohen,Mononito Goswami,Junhong Shen,Emaad Khwaja,Chenghao Liu,David Asker,Othmane Abou-Amal,Ameet Talwalkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARFBench，一个面向时间序列异常检测的多模态问答基准，评估基础模型在生产环境软件事件数据上的理解能力，并展示了多模态方法与人类专家互补可达到超人类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列问答（TSQA）是基础模型有潜力但尚未被充分探索的能力，现有评估缺乏针对软件事件中时间序列异常的多模态基准。

Method: 构建包含750个问题、142个时间序列、538万数据点、来自Datadog 63个生产事件的ARFBench基准；评估多种LLM、VLM和时间序列FM；开发TSFM+VLM混合原型并进行后训练；定义模型-专家oracle选择器。

Result: 前沿VLM表现最佳（GPT-5达62.7%准确率、51.9% F1）；混合原型在少量数据后训练后达到与前沿模型相当的性能；模型-专家oracle达82.8% F1和87.2%准确率，超越人类单独表现。

Conclusion: ARFBench揭示了当前模型在TSQA任务上的局限性，验证了多模态融合的潜力，并表明模型与人类专家存在互补优势，为未来TSQA模型设立了新的超人类基准。

Abstract: Time series question-answering (TSQA), in which we ask natural language questions to infer and reason about properties of time series, is a promising yet underexplored capability of foundation models. In this work, we present ARFBench, a TSQA benchmark that evaluates the understanding of multimodal foundation models (FMs) on time series anomalies prevalent in software incident data. ARFBench consists of 750 questions across 142 time series and 5.38M data points from 63 production incidents sourced exclusively from internal telemetry at Datadog. We evaluate leading proprietary and open-source LLMs, VLMs, and time series FMs and observe that frontier VLMs perform markedly better than existing baselines; the leading model (GPT-5) achieves a 62.7% accuracy and 51.9% F1. We next demonstrate the promise of specialized multimodal approaches. We develop a novel TSFM + VLM hybrid prototype which we post-train on a small set of synthetic and real data that yields comparable overall F1 and accuracy with frontier models. Lastly, we find models and human domain experts exhibit complementary strengths. We define a model-expert oracle, a best-of-2 oracle selector over model and expert answers, yielding 82.8% F1 and 87.2% accuracy and establishing a new superhuman frontier for future TSQA models. The benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Datadog/ARFBench.

</details>


### [173] [The Recurrent Transformer: Greater Effective Depth and Efficient Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21215)
*Costin-Andrei Oncescu,Depen Morwani,Samy Jelassi,Alexandru Meterez,Mujin Kwun,Sham Kakade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出循环Transformer架构，每层关注自身激活产生的键值对以实现层循环记忆，并给出基于tiling的精确算法将HBM流量从Θ(N²)降至Θ(N log N)。实验表明该架构在150M和300M参数上优于参数匹配的Transformer基线，可用更少层数实现更好性能，降低KV缓存和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer并行处理但时间深度受限（深度等于层数），循环模型理论上可提供无限时间深度但优化不稳定且难以充分利用现代加速器。希望结合两者优势。

Method: 架构上，每层关注基于自身激活（而非前一层）计算出的键值对，实现逐层循环记忆。针对预训练/训练中因键值顺序暴露导致带宽受限的问题，提出基于tiling的精确算法，将HBM流量从Θ(N²)降至Θ(N log N)，算术强度从约1提升至Θ(N/log N)。

Result: 在150M和300M参数的C4预训练任务上，循环Transformer在固定参数量下以更少层数实现了优于参数匹配Transformer基线的交叉熵，表明循环性可用深度换取宽度，降低KV缓存和推理延迟。

Conclusion: 循环Transformer是一种简单有效的架构改进，在保持标准自回归解码成本的同时，通过层循环记忆提升了性能，并可用更少层数达到更好效果，具有实际部署优势。

Abstract: Transformers process tokens in parallel but are temporally shallow: at position $t$, each layer attends to key-value pairs computed based on the previous layer, yielding a depth capped by the number of layers. Recurrent models offer unbounded temporal depth but suffer from optimization instability and historically underutilize modern accelerators. We introduce the Recurrent Transformer, a simple architectural change where each layer attends to key-value pairs computed off its own activations, yielding layerwise recurrent memory while preserving standard autoregressive decoding cost. We show that the architecture can emulate both (i) a conventional Transformer and (ii) token-to-token recurrent updates under mild assumptions, while avoiding optimization instability. Naively, prefill/training appears bandwidth-bound with effective arithmetic intensity near $1$ because keys and values are revealed sequentially; we give an exact tiling-based algorithm that preserves the mathematical computation while reducing HBM traffic from $Θ(N^2)$ to $Θ(N\log N)$, increasing effective arithmetic intensity to $Θ(N/\log N)$ for sequence length $N$. On 150M and 300M parameter C4 pretraining, Recurrent Transformers improve cross-entropy over a parameter-matched Transformer baseline and achieve the improvement with fewer layers (fixed parameters), suggesting that recurrence can trade depth for width, thus reducing KV cache memory footprint and inference latency.

</details>


### [174] [Learning Dynamic Representations and Policies from Multimodal Clinical Time-Series with Informative Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21235)
*Zihan Liang,Ziwen Pan,Ruoxuan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个多模态临床时间序列的患者表征学习框架，通过显式利用信息性缺失（informative missingness）来整合结构化测量和临床笔记中的观测模式，并在ICU脓毒症数据集上改善了离线治疗策略学习和不良结局预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽然能处理临床时间序列中的缺失值，但如何提取和利用观测过程本身携带的信息（即信息性缺失）仍未被充分探索，尤其是多模态数据中不同模态的观测模式存在差异。

Method: 框架包含三个模块：(1) 多模态编码器，捕捉结构化数据和文本数据及其观测模式信号；(2) 贝叶斯滤波模块，根据观测到的多模态信号随时间更新潜在患者状态；(3) 下游模块，基于学习到的患者状态进行离线治疗策略学习和患者结局预测。

Result: 在MIMIC-III、MIMIC-IV和eICU的ICU脓毒症队列上评估，离线治疗策略学习FQE达0.679（vs. 临床医生行为0.528），MIMIC-III上72小时死亡率预测AUROC达0.886。

Conclusion: 显式利用多模态观测过程中的信息性缺失可以有效提升患者表征学习的质量，从而改善离线治疗策略和预后预测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical records contain structured measurements and clinical notes recorded over time, offering rich temporal information about the evolution of patient health. Yet these observations are sparse, and whether they are recorded depends on the patient's latent condition. Observation patterns also differ across modalities, as structured measurements and clinical notes arise under distinct recording processes. While prior work has developed methods that accommodate missingness in clinical time series, how to extract and use the information carried by the observation process itself remains underexplored. We therefore propose a patient representation learning framework for multimodal clinical time series that explicitly leverages informative missingness. The framework combines (1) a multimodal encoder that captures signals from structured and textual data together with their observation patterns, (2) a Bayesian filtering module that updates a latent patient state over time from observed multimodal signals, and (3) downstream modules for offline treatment policy learning and patient outcome prediction based on the learned patient state. We evaluate the framework on ICU sepsis cohorts from MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, and eICU. It improves both offline treatment policy learning and adverse outcome prediction, achieving FQE 0.679 versus 0.528 for clinician behavior and AUROC 0.886 for post-72-hour mortality prediction on MIMIC-III.

</details>


### [175] [CAP: Controllable Alignment Prompting for Unlearning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21251)
*Zhaokun Wang,Jinyu Guo,Jingwen Pu,Hongli Pu,Meng Yang,Xunlei Chen,Jie Ou,Wenyi Li,Guangchun Luo,Wenhong Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAP框架通过强化学习驱动的可学习提示优化，实现了无需修改模型参数的、可逆且可控的知识遗忘，解决了现有参数修改方法的高成本、遗忘边界不可控及对模型权重访问的依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数修改方法在知识遗忘任务中面临计算成本高、遗忘边界不可控、严重依赖模型权重访问等根本性限制，这些约束使其不适用于闭源模型，而现有的非侵入式替代方法缺乏系统性且依赖经验。

Method: 提出CAP框架，将知识遗忘解耦为通过强化学习进行的可学习提示优化过程，其中提示生成器与大语言模型协作，在抑制目标知识的同时选择性保留通用能力，并通过提示撤销实现可逆的知识恢复。

Result: 大量实验表明，CAP在不更新模型参数的情况下实现了精确、可控的知识遗忘，建立了一种克服先前方法可迁移性限制的动态对齐机制。

Conclusion: CAP框架通过端到端的提示驱动遗忘范式，有效解决了参数修改方法的局限性，为闭源模型提供了一种可逆、可控且无需权重访问的知识遗忘方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) trained on unfiltered corpora inherently risk retaining sensitive information, necessitating selective knowledge unlearning for regulatory compliance and ethical safety. However, existing parameter-modifying methods face fundamental limitations: high computational costs, uncontrollable forgetting boundaries, and strict dependency on model weight access. These constraints render them impractical for closed-source models, yet current non-invasive alternatives remain unsystematic and reliant on empirical experience. To address these challenges, we propose the Controllable Alignment Prompting for Unlearning (CAP) framework, an end-to-end prompt-driven unlearning paradigm. CAP decouples unlearning into a learnable prompt optimization process via reinforcement learning, where a prompt generator collaborates with the LLM to suppress target knowledge while preserving general capabilities selectively. This approach enables reversible knowledge restoration through prompt revocation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CAP achieves precise, controllable unlearning without updating model parameters, establishing a dynamic alignment mechanism that overcomes the transferability limitations of prior methods.

</details>


### [176] [Improving Performance in Classification Tasks with LCEN and the Weighted Focal Differentiable MCC Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21252)
*Pedro Seber,Richard D. Braatz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LASSO-Clip-EN (LCEN)算法从回归任务扩展到分类任务，并引入加权可微差分马修斯相关系数(diffMCC)损失函数。实验表明，LCEN在四个分类数据集上优于大多数对比模型，平均消除56%的特征；diffMCC损失函数相比加权交叉熵损失，平均macro F1提高4.9%，MCC提高8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCEN算法此前仅适用于回归任务，不具备分类能力。作者旨在将其扩展至分类任务，同时保持其非线性、可解释性和特征选择等优良特性，并进一步探索更优的损失函数以提升分类性能。

Method: 1. 修改LCEN算法使其适用于分类任务；2. 在四个二分类和多分类数据集上，将LCEN与10种其他模型进行对比；3. 引入加权可微差分马修斯相关系数(diffMCC)损失函数；4. 使用LCEN选择的特征重训练所有模型，评估性能提升。

Result: LCEN在分类任务中达到较高的macro F1和MCC，优于大多数对比模型；平均消除56%的输入特征；基于LCEN选择特征重训练后，三个实验出现统计显著的性能提升；diffMCC损失函数在所有实验中均表现最佳，宏F1平均提高4.9%，MCC平均提高8.5%。

Conclusion: LCEN算法成功扩展至分类任务，兼具特征选择和机器学习能力；diffMCC损失函数可训练出比加权交叉熵损失更精确的模型，在分类任务中具有显著优势。

Abstract: The LASSO-Clip-EN (LCEN) algorithm was previously introduced for nonlinear, interpretable feature selection and machine learning. However, its design and use was limited to regression tasks. In this work, we create a modified version of the LCEN algorithm that is suitable for classification tasks and maintains its desirable properties, such as interpretability. This modified LCEN algorithm is evaluated on four widely used binary and multiclass classification datasets. In these experiments, LCEN is compared against 10 other model types and consistently reaches high test-set macro F$_1$ score and Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) metrics, higher than that of the majority of investigated models. LCEN models for classification remain sparse, eliminating an average of 56% of all input features in the experiments performed. Furthermore, LCEN-selected features are used to retrain all models using the same data, leading to statistically significant performance improvements in three of the experiments and insignificant differences in the fourth when compared to using all features or other feature selection methods. Simultaneously, the weighted focal differentiable MCC (diffMCC) loss function is evaluated on the same datasets. Models trained with the diffMCC loss function are always the best-performing methods in these experiments, and reach test-set macro F$_1$ scores that are, on average, 4.9% higher and MCCs that are 8.5% higher than those obtained by models trained with the weighted cross-entropy loss. These results highlight the performance of LCEN as a feature selection and machine learning algorithm also for classification tasks, and how the diffMCC loss function can train very accurate models, surpassing the weighted cross-entropy loss in the tasks investigated.

</details>


### [177] [Hyperloop Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21254)
*Abbas Zeitoun,Lucas Torroba-Hennigen,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hyperloop Transformer架构，通过循环Transformer层（仅中间块循环）和超连接（hyper-connections）技术，在参数量减少约50%的情况下，性能超越深度匹配的普通Transformer和mHC Transformer基线，且优势在量化后依然保持，适合内存高效的语言建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM架构研究通常以固定计算/延迟预算下最大化模型质量为目标，但边缘设备和端侧部署等应用场景还受到模型内存占用的约束，因此需要参数高效的架构。

Method: 提出Hyperloop Transformer架构：1) 将循环Transformer组织为三个块（开始块、中间块、结束块），仅中间块循环复用；2) 在每次循环后引入超连接（hyper-connections），将残差流扩展为矩阵值残差流，仅增加极少的参数和计算成本。

Result: 在不同模型规模下，Hyperloop Transformer在参数量减少约50%的情况下，性能优于深度匹配的普通Transformer和mHC Transformer基线，且优势在权重量化后依然保持。

Conclusion: Hyperloop Transformer是一种具有吸引力的内存高效语言建模架构。

Abstract: LLM architecture research generally aims to maximize model quality subject to fixed compute/latency budgets. However, many applications of interest such as edge and on-device deployment are further constrained by the model's memory footprint, thus motivating parameter-efficient architectures for language modeling. This paper describes a simple architecture that improves the parameter-efficiency of LLMs. Our architecture makes use of looped Transformers as a core primitive, which reuse Transformer layers across depth and are thus more parameter-efficient than ordinary (depth-matched) Transformers. We organize the looped Transformer into three blocks--begin, middle, and end blocks--where each block itself consists of multiple Transformer layers, and only the middle block is applied recurrently across depth. We augment the looped middle block with hyper-connections (Xie et al., 2026), which expand the residual stream into matrix-valued residual streams. Hyper-connections are applied only after each loop, and therefore add minimal new parameters and compute cost. Across various model scales, we find that our Hyper-Connected Looped Transformer (Hyperloop Transformer) is able to outperform depth-matched Transformer and mHC Transformer baselines despite using approximately 50% fewer parameters. The outperformance persists through post-training weight quantization, thus positioning Hyperloop Transformers as an attractive architecture for memory-efficient language modeling.

</details>


### [178] [Measure Twice, Click Once: Co-evolving Proposer and Visual Critic via Reinforcement Learning for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21268)
*Wenkai Wang,Xiyun Li,Hongcan Guo,Wenhao Yu,Tianqing Fang,Haitao Mi,Dong Yu,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种可学习的Propose-then-Critic框架，通过成熟度感知的自适应协同进化强化学习，动态平衡定位器与评判器的训练目标，显著提升GUI grounding精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI grounding方法在视觉同质元素和密集布局下难以精确定位；静态自一致性策略（基于几何聚类）改进有限，因模型预测呈空间分散分布。

Method: 提出Propose-then-Critic框架：用可学习的选择机制替代静态一致性策略，通过评判器对自身渲染在截图上的提案进行批判来选择最优目标；采用成熟度感知的自适应协同进化强化学习，动态平衡提议者和评判者的训练目标，实现两者互促共进。

Result: 在6个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了grounding准确率和评判器可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过可学习的协同进化机制，使提议者的输出多样性与评判器的判别能力相互增强，从而泛化适应多样化的复杂界面布局。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding requires mapping natural language instructions to precise pixel coordinates. However, due to visually homogeneous elements and dense layouts, models typically grasp semantic intent yet struggle with achieving precise localization. While scaling sampling attempts (Pass@k) reveals potential gains, static self-consistency strategies derived from geometric clustering often yield limited improvements, as the model's predictions tend to be spatially dispersed. In this paper, we propose replacing static consistency strategies with a learnable selection mechanism that selects the optimal target by critiquing its own proposals rendered on the screenshot. Given the significant disparity between the model's grounding and critiquing capabilities, we propose a co-evolving Propose-then-Critic framework. To jointly optimize these, we introduce a maturity-aware adaptive co-evolutionary reinforcement learning paradigm. This approach dynamically balances the training objectives of proposer and critic, where the diversity of the proposer's outputs enhances critic robustness, while the critic's maturing discrimination capability conversely unlocks the proposer's potential for extensive spatial exploration, fostering the mutual reinforcement and co-evolution of both capabilities, thereby ensuring generalizability to adapt to diverse and complex interface layouts. Extensive experiments over 6 benchmarks show that our method significantly enhances both grounding accuracy and critic reliability.

</details>


### [179] [Understanding and Mitigating Spurious Signal Amplification in Test-Time Reinforcement Learning for Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21327)
*Yongcan Yu,Lingxiao He,Jian Liang,Kuangpu Guo,Meng Wang,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对测试时强化学习（TTRL）中标签噪声导致的虚假优化信号问题，提出统一框架DDRL，通过频率采样排除模糊样本、去偏优势估计和基于共识的离策略精炼三个阶段，在多个数学推理基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTRL在推理时通过伪标签进行模型自适应，但容易受到标签噪声产生的虚假优化信号影响。现有方法对此缺乏有效应对。

Method: DDRL包含三个阶段：1) 频率采样策略排除中等一致性（模糊区域）样本，同时保持正负样本平衡；2) 采用固定优势值的去偏优势估计，消除组相对策略优化引入的偏差；3) 基于共识的离策略精炼阶段，利用拒绝采样数据集实现高效稳定的模型更新。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上，使用三种大型语言模型进行实验，DDRL一致优于现有TTRL基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过针对性处理模糊样本和去偏优势估计，DDRL有效缓解了TTRL中标签噪声带来的虚假信号问题，提升了推理时自适应的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) always adapts models at inference time via pseudo-labeling, leaving it vulnerable to spurious optimization signals from label noise. Through an empirical study, we observe that responses with medium consistency form an ambiguity region and constitute the primary source of reward noise. Crucially, we find that such spurious signals can be even amplified through group-relative advantage estimation. Motivated by these findings, we propose a unified framework, Debiased and Denoised test-time Reinforcement Learning (DDRL), to mitigate spurious signals. Concretely, DDRL first applies a frequency-based sampling strategy to exclude ambiguous samples while maintaining a balanced set of positive and negative examples. It then adopts a debiased advantage estimation with fixed advantages, removing the bias introduced by group-relative policy optimization. Finally, DDRL incorporates a consensus-based off-policy refinement stage, which leverages the rejection-sampled dataset to enable efficient and stable model updates. Experiments on three large language models across multiple mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that DDRL consistently outperforms existing TTRL baselines. The code will soon be released at https://github.com/yuyongcan/DDRL.

</details>


### [180] [Sub-Token Routing in LoRA for Adaptation and Query-Aware KV Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21335)
*Wei Jiang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LoRA适配Transformer中的子令牌（sub-token）路由机制，提出两种设计：查询无关的压缩感知语言建模方案和查询相关的下游任务保持KV压缩方案，实验表明子令牌级路由能比令牌级路由提供更精细的压缩控制，两者互为补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统令牌级KV压缩是"全有或全无"的粗粒度决策，但相关令牌内部并非均匀一致，保留的价值组在令牌间和令牌内分布不均，因此需要在子令牌粒度进行更精细的路由控制以优化压缩效率。

Method: 提出两种子令牌路由机制：(1) 查询无关设计：结合路由子空间LoRA与值组路由，用于压缩感知语言建模；(2) 查询相关设计：基于预测器的选择器根据查询条件相关性，在上下文令牌/值组对上分配全局保留预算，用于下游任务保持的KV压缩。

Result: 查询无关设计在语言建模的质量-压缩权衡上优于基线；查询相关设计在降低KV预算下能保持下游任务性能。实验还揭示令牌级与子令牌级路由是互补的压缩轴：令牌级决定哪些令牌全局存活，子令牌级决定存活令牌如何内部压缩。

Conclusion: 子令牌路由提供了比令牌、页面、头或层更精细的Transformer效率控制轴，令牌级与子令牌级路由在不同粒度上协同工作，共同优化压缩效果。

Abstract: Sub-token routing offers a finer control axis for transformer efficiency than the coarse units used in most prior work, such as tokens, pages, heads, or layers. In this paper, we study routing within a token representation itself in LoRA-adapted transformers. The motivation is that a relevant token need not be internally uniform: under a retention budget, preserved value groups are distributed unevenly both across tokens and within tokens, which suggests that KV compression need not be an all-or-nothing decision at token level.
